# End

**Author**: Controverse judéo-chrétienne  
**Series**: PO Tome 13  
**Columns**: pp. 5–96  

[p. 5] {#po.13.p5}

### SARGIS OF ABERGA
"ዳግም ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፪መዋፅል ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፡ ናሁ ፣ ክርስቲ ያን ፡ሀሰዉ ፡ ወዓቢየ ፡ ስሕቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እፎ ፡ ይትከሀል ፡ ከመዝ ፣ ብሂል ፡ ሐሰ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ከመ ፡ ኢነሥአ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ወወለደ ፣ እምኔሃ ፤ ወልደ ፡ ኢመፍትውኩኬ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወልደ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡5 አላ ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ ርቱዕ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ መሲሕ ፡ ወመድኅን ፡ ወገረዳኢ ፡ ወ ማሕየዊ
* SECOND ASSEMBLY 56. Christ the Son of the Lord. - Two days later, they gathered again. They said to Jacob: "Behold, the Christians lie and err greatly, for they say that Christ is the Son of the Lord. How is it possible to say such a thing? * Far be it from the Lord to have taken a wife and to have begotten a son from her! One must not, therefore, say that Christ is the Son of the Lord. But it is only just to say that he is the Messiah, the Savior, the Helper, and the Vivifier."
ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ትካት ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ አነሂ ፣ እንዘ ፣ እሔሊ ፡ ከመዝ ፣ ወብዙ ኀ ፡ ለያልየ ፡ በከይኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እስእል ፡ ወአንቆ[ዐ]ሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያለብወኒ' ፡ ዘንተ ፡፡ ወናሁ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ በሕልም ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ዘይለብስ ፡ ጸዓድወ ፡ አልባሰ ፡
Jacob said to them: "Formerly, I too continued to think such a thing. (But) for many nights I wept, asking and lifting (my soul) toward the Lord, so that He might make me understand this. And behold, I saw in a dream a man who was clothed in white garments and was saying to me: "Why

do you refuse to say that Christ is the Son of the Lord? Have you not heard what the prophet David says: I have begotten you from the womb before the morning star. Furthermore, he says concerning the birth (of Christ) according to the flesh: The Lord * said to me: You are my Son. I myself have begotten you today. Ask of me; I will give you the nations for your inheritance and your dominion shall be to the ends of the earth. Moreover, the Scripture says: The Lord says: His reign shall not end; he has established his throne in justice, in Jerusalem, forever. The brothers answered and said to him: "Behold, you yourself have not understood this word, for what you saw in a dream is expounded concerning Solomon, son of David." Jacob said: "As for me, I have not lied, but on the contrary, I have spoken the truth: (namely) that what was said: I have begotten you from the womb before the morning star (was indeed said) concerning (Christ). He himself existed before the world was created. As for his dominion, it is to the ends of the earth. Solomon, * for his part, did not exist before the morning star; his reign was not eternal, but on the contrary, he reigned for 40 years, as it is written in the Scriptures. (It is also said) how he was deposed from his kingship and (how) he did evil before the Lord. The prophet David says: O Lord,

give your judgment to the king and your justice to the king's son. Furthermore, he says: Before the sun his name existed; before the moon it was for generations of generations. The prophet Hosea says: Out of Egypt I have called my Son. Solomon also says concerning Christ: The Lord created me [Wisdom] (as) the beginning of the way of his throne. Before the world he founded me. (He created me) in the beginning, before he * made the earth, before the springs of water came forth, before the mountains were founded. Before all the hills he begot me. The Lord had (not yet) made the regions and the poles which he established from the sky. When he prepared the heavens, I was there. When he created his own throne in the air, when he made it solid above the clouds, when he strengthened the location of the springs of water, when he set a limit to the sea, (so that) the waters would not go out of their shore, (when he) made solid the foundations of the earth, I was with him, (arranging) (all things). I myself rejoiced every day (and) exulted before his face, all the time. Furthermore, he says: Who has ascended * to heaven and descended (from it)? Who has gathered the winds in his bosom? Who has wrapped the waters in his garment? What is his name and what is the name of his Son? (Can you) know it? One of the brothers, named Theodore,


[p. 8] {#po.13.p8}

57. Abolition of the Jewish priesthood. - Jacob said: "Do you not know, brothers, that the priesthood of the children of Israel has been abolished before the Lord?" The brothers answered and said to him: "Because of what?" Jacob said to them: "Because we have transgressed the Law of Moses that the Lord, our God, had given us. That is why the priesthood was taken from us, as it is written in the Book * of Kings: A man of the Lord came to Eli and said to him: Thus says the Lord: Indeed, I manifested myself to your father's house, while they were in the land of Egypt (and) while they were slaves of Pharaoh. I chose your father's house from among all the houses of Israel, so that they might become priests for me, that they might ascend to my altar, that they might burn incense, and that they might wear the ephod. I gave to your father's house all (the sacrifices) that are treated by fire, (offered) by the children of Israel. Why with an evil eye have you looked at my sacrifice and my incense, and have preferred your sons to me regarding the blessing of the firstfruits of all the sacrifices of Israel that were before me? * That is why the Lord, God of Israel, says thus: I had said that your house and your father's house would serve before me forever. But now, says the Lord, far from

me! I will honor only those who honor me and I will despise the one who despises me. Behold: the days are coming (when) I will uproot your house and the descendants of your father's house. There will no longer be found for you an old man in your house at any time. A man (however) will not be removed from my sacrifice: the one who will be perfect in my eyes and will desire (my glory) in his soul. All those who remain of your house will fall by the sword of men. Behold the sign for you: it is what will happen to your two * sons Hophni and Phinehas; they will both die, (struck) by the sword, on the same day.

Have you seen, brothers, how the ancient Law and the priesthood of the people of the Jews have been abolished? The light has come that the prophet predicted concerning the coming of Christ: I will raise up faithful priests according to my will, who will do my good pleasure; I will build for them a house of justice; they will walk in my commandment, all the days of their life. Furthermore, the Lord says to the priest Eli: Those who remain of your house will come to prostrate themselves before him, (to have) a small silver coin, and will say to him: Join me to one of your priests, so that I may eat food. Moreover, the Lord says * to Samuel: Behold, I myself will fulfill my word concerning Israel, so that every being who hears this will stop his ears. That day, I will execute all that

I have said against Eli and against his house; I will begin and I will finish. I notified him that I myself will punish his house forever, because of the sin of his sons (whom he) did not chastise. Have I not precisely sworn to his house that the sin of the house of Eli will not be erased, neither by incense, nor by sacrifice, forever? The brothers answered and said to him: "You speak the truth, Jacob, for behold, such a thing was said concerning Saul."

58. The Trinity. - Isaac answered and said: "Only one thing remains for us to ask you. Must we adore the Holy Trinity and believe * in it, as the Christians say?" Jacob answered and said to them: "The Law and the prophets teach and announce the Holy Trinity. The Law says: Let us make man in our image and in our likeness. It is the Father who conversed with his Son and his Holy Spirit. Furthermore, Moses says in the Law: The Lord, your God, is one. Thus is explained (the formula): The Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, one single God. Again he says: I am the God of Abraham, the God of Isaac, and the God of Jacob. Behold, he mentioned the Holy Trinity and said: God of Abraham, of Isaac, and of Jacob, before stating, concerning the Holy Trinity, that it is one single God. Moreover, it is expounded in the Scriptures that the Virtues of the heavens, * the Angels themselves, the Seraphim

Jesus, son of Sirach, says: I have called the Lord my light. The Law says: * The Spirit of the Lord hovered above the water. Furthermore, it says: The Lord made rain upon Sodom and Gomorrah fire and sulfur from the Lord, (from) heaven. The prophet Isaiah says: They have irritated the Holy Spirit. The Law says: Jacob took three rods and put them in the water, in order to make Laban's sheep drink. Behold the sign of (this) prophecy: the sheep are the faithful who believe in Christ; the water is the sign of the holy baptism which expunges sin; the three rods that (Jacob) put in the water are

the sign of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit who sanctifies the water of baptism. It is by it that the sheep of the Lord are purified. * As the prophet Ezekiel says: Behold, I myself will reward my sheep; my sheep will know that I am the Lord, their God. Furthermore, he says: Thus says the Lord: Behold, I will seek my sheep and gather them. Just as the shepherd seeks his sheep and his flock on the day of darkness and clouds, (and is) in the midst of his sheep that are separated, so I will seek my sheep and review them, (after having brought them back) from the regions where they were scattered. (Moses) says in the Law: Come, let us descend and mix their language. Furthermore, he says: Behold, the Lord has ordered the children of Israel to go to the desert (and to make) a three-day journey, in order to render worship to the Lord, their God. By that, he wanted to make them understand the mystery of the vivifying Holy Trinity. (The reason) for which he says: * three days, is that it is the Holy Trinity (that) brought our Fathers out of the land of Egypt. The prophet David says: By the Word of the Lord the heavens were strengthened and the Spirit of his mouth is all their strength. Furthermore, he says: He sent his Word and he healed them. Moreover, he says: You will send your Spirit and (all things) will be created.

The people of Nineveh fasted for three days and were saved from the punishment of his wrath. The prophet Elijah made the sign of the Trinity on the altar and fire descended from heaven. Indeed, he threw water on the altar three times (and) made the mystery of the Trinity seen. The fire that descended from heaven is the sign of the Spirit, consumption of the operation of the Trinity: Father, Son, and Holy Spirit. It is it that sanctifies baptism, and * all those who are baptized are purified by it. It is also the sign of the cross, of the Incarnation of Christ, and of the celestial light that sanctifies those who are baptized. The prophet Daniel made, in his house, three windows; he fasted for three weeks; he prayed (three times), before the Lord (who) heard his prayer and satisfied his request. By that, he teaches us the mystery of the Trinity. Indeed, it is by it that everything was created. As the prophet David says: You will send your Spirit and (all things) will be created. Furthermore, he says: Offer to the Lord a sacrifice of glory and render to the Most High your vow. Invoke me on the day of your affliction; I will deliver you and you will render me thanks.

end, (you before whom) the angels stand in fear, (you) at whose command the wind and fire occur, (you) whose voice is strong, (you) whose word remains (forever), (you) whose decree is powerful and fearsome. The Virtues of the heavens say to you: Holy, holy, holy; the spirits of the just glorify you. You grant (your) benefits to the pious (people) who love you. Every living being adores you. Without you nothing exists. Indeed, you are the Lord. You have established a Mediator by the work of your Wisdom. Likewise, the Holy Spirit illuminates the eyes of the heart of the saints. As for Wisdom, he embraces it, he who is equal to it by the will of the * Most High Lord.

O brothers, Ezra said that concerning the Trinity. Again he says: Save us, O Lord, from the hands of the wicked; in the greatness of your clemency and in your goodness look toward us; by (your) powerful Spirit help us and strengthen us. Yes, O Lord, we pray (to you) and we lift our soul toward you. O Friend of men, O Messiah, O Sweet, O Clement, O Merciful, turn toward us; save us; lift your face upon us; make luminous our souls and our intelligences, like purified gold that has been melted seven times; purify us by the water of justice, so that we may be purified from the defilement of our souls and our bodies; by your own purity purify us; sustain us by

your strong arm and by your uplifted right hand; make us walk in your path, in your good pleasure; enlighten us with the light of your face. * This, O brothers, is the testimony of Ezra concerning the Holy Trinity and the purification of baptism. (Ezra) published the name of Christ manifestly. The prophet David says: Have mercy on me and hear me, my heart says to you. I have sought your face; I seek your own face, O Lord; do not turn your face from me. This triple (word): face, which the prophet mentioned, relates to the Holy Trinity. The prophet Zechariah says: Rejoice and exult, daughter of Zion, for I myself will come and I will dwell in the midst of you, says the Lord. Many nations will take refuge with the Lord; that day, they will become (my) people and will dwell in the midst of you. You will know that the Lord who rules over all has sent me to you. Furthermore he says: I will pour out upon the house of David and upon those who dwell in Jerusalem a spirit of absolution, * of joy and of gladness.

Have you seen, O * brothers, how the prophets have expounded and preached, in the Holy Scriptures, concerning the Holy Trinity?" The brothers answered and said to him: "It is the way of justice and of faith. Now blessed is he who walks in it: he will live forever. But for those who repudiate it, their end will be in the eternal torment which will have (neither) term, nor completion. Indeed, the Law and all the prophets have prophesied concerning the Holy Trinity (and have said) that the Lord of all (things) is the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit who spoke by the mouth of his prophets."

59. The present meeting of the Jews is secret. - Then I, Joseph, said to Jacob: * "Beg the brothers to (allow) us to write what we have said among ourselves, so that it may be useful for us and also for those who will come after us. Behold then that the truth and the faith have been revealed to us." Jacob said to them: "We will not make our exposition heard by the Christians, for behold we have entered into the New Testament. Christians, for their part, believe in Christ with uprightness; much better than us they know his Law and his Testament; furthermore they have Scriptures which do not exist among us; they have science, wisdom, and knowledge of the Law and the Testament. Behold we see that they curse those who violate their Law and their Testament, and furthermore that they excommunicate from the Church and call apostates those who have violated the rule. Indeed, the Holy Spirit teaches them what we do not know ourselves. * As the prophet Joel says: I will pour out of my Spirit upon all flesh; your sons and your daughters will prophesy. The prophet Isaiah says concerning the people of the Christians: (It) will be blessed in the land that the Lord Sabaoth has blessed. He said: Blessed is my people who are in Egypt and who are in Persia. Israel, for its part, is my inheritance. As for us, brothers, people of the Jews, we have not recognized as accurate the faith of the Christians, (a fact) which they do not understand. Behold we see the patriarchs and the bishops excommunicating the men of their people who have stumbled off the way of Christ. Indeed, they themselves do not receive (into the community) those who do not have the (orthodox) faith."
አውሥኡ ፡ አኅው ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ መንከረ" ፡ ይሜኒ ፡ ትም”ሀርትከክ ፡ ኦአነ ፡=፡ ወ

beautiful, O our brother. However, we ask you to continue to (explain) to us the words that the prophets have preached concerning the coming of Our Lord Jesus Christ. Indeed, behold we see that you possess many testimonies (drawn) from the Scriptures, on this subject." He answered and said to them: "Yes, I (am going to) expound (that) to you. The prophet Isaiah says: Behold I myself will place in Zion a stone of stumbling, that is to say, the Christ who has become a stumbling block for the Jews who were scandalized by him. As Job the Just says concerning those who have not received Christ: The Lord will not save the wicked and the impious. Indeed, they did not want to see the Lord. The prophet Isaiah says: Their way is tortuous. The prophet Joel * says: Will you then exercise vengeance against me? Will you prepare vengeance against me? Quickly and rapidly I will return (your vengeance) upon your head. The prophet Isaiah says: They have gone into darkness, so as not to see the light. The prophet Hosea says concerning the Jews who do not believe in Christ: With reason the Lord has rejected them, for they refused to listen to him; they will become (most) of them wanderers among the peoples. The prophet Jeremiah says: Behold I myself will bring

misfortune upon my people according to the fruit of their works, for they did not pay attention to my words and they have transgressed my Law.

Furthermore Jacob said to them: "O brothers, behold we have been scandalized (concerning) Christ and we have not believed in him. Indeed, from the time that * Our Lord Jesus Christ came until the present epoch of the reign of King Heraclius, there are 600 years. Behold we have been dispersed and reproved among all the peoples. The punishment has come upon us. Those who believe in Christ have made us captives. Satan has enslaved us and has made us his own people. 61. Belief in Christ. - The prophet Isaiah says: The Lord will come in strength. Furthermore he says: That day, the deaf will hear the word of the Scripture; those who are in darkness (will be enlightened); the eyes of the blind will see; the poor will rejoice in the Lord with gladness; the desperate people will be satiated with joy in the Holy One of Israel. Moreover he says on this subject: Thus says the Lord to the house of Jacob whom he brought out of the house of Abraham: It is not now that Jacob will be confounded; * it is not now that his face will be upset. When his children see my work, (accomplished) by me, they will sanctify my name; they will sanctify (the God of) the house

of Jacob; they will also fear the God of Israel. Those whose spirit was led astray will know the wisdom (of the Lord); those also who murmured will learn (his) Law. Again he says: That day, those who will have remained of Israel and those who will have been saved of Jacob will no longer continue to trust in those who led them astray, but they will trust in the Lord, the Holy One of Israel, with justice. Those who will have remained will lean upon the Lord. When the number of the Israelites would be like the sand of the sea, those who will have remained will be saved (only), for the Lord will make an extermination and a cutting off * with justice in the whole world. Indeed, the family of men has remained in sadness and affliction, in soul and (in) body. Behold the Lord has healed them and has made peace between them. Those who followed the impure gods and served them, he has saved. He has taught the peoples, so that they might recount the truth and the accuracy concerning the coming of Christ. Indeed, he himself is the giver of peace and the reconciler for all those who believe in him. That is why they will be saved; they will inherit eternal life and they will glorify the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit."

ዘትካት ፡ ወዘቀዲሙ ፣ ኢትሔልዩ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አነ ፡፣ እገብር ፡ ሐዲሰ ፡ ዘይእዜ ፡ ይሰርቅ፣0 ከመ ፣ ታእምሩ ፡ እገብር ፡ ፍኖት ፣ ውስተ ፡ በድው ፣ ወአውሕዝ › ማየ ፣ ውስተ፣ ገዳም ።፡ ወይድኅሩኒ፤ ፡ አራዊተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወእጓለ ፡ አዕዋፍ ፡ ወሴሬኔስ ፣ እስመ ፡ ወ ሀብኩ ፡፤ ማየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በድው ፣ ወአውሐዝኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ከመ ፡ አስትዮሙ፣። ለሕዝብየ ፡ ወለኅሩያንየ ፡ አለ ፣ አጥረይኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግሩ ፡ ስብሐትየ ፡ ወይግበሩ ፡ ትእዛዝየ ናሁ ፣ ዝውእቱ ፡ ኦአኀው ፡ ትንቢት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጥምቀት" ።፡ ወበእንተ ፣ አለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ ወ፲ዘነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ጽሙኣን ፣ አማነ ፡=፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢመፍትው" ፡ ንሕየው ፡ በሕገ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ መጽአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ።፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእነግር ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ ወአየድዕ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ወንዑ ፡ ወተማከሩ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ እለ ፡ ድኅንክሙ ፡ እምሕዝብ ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፣ አነኬ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ባዕደ ፡ ዘእንበሌየ'፣ ጻድቅ ፣፤ ወማሕየዊ ፡ ወአልቦ ፣ ከማየ ፡ ተመየጡ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ወተሐይዉ ፡ እለ ፣ እምአጽ ናፈ ፡ ምድር ። አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ባዕደ ፡ ዘእንበሌየ" =።፡ መሐል
prophet Isaiah concerning those who will have believed in Christ: You will no longer remember the things of old * and you will no longer think of the things of yore. Behold I myself will do a new thing which (is going to) manifest itself now, so that you may know (it). I will make a path in the desert and I will make water flow in the solitude. The wild beasts, the birds, and the winged creatures will bless me, for I have given water in the desert and I have made (water) flow in the solitude, in order to water my people and my elect whom I have acquired, to recount my glory and accomplish my orders. Behold this is, O brothers, the prophecy concerning baptism and concerning those who have believed (and) who have remained thirsty for truth. Furthermore he says that we must not live according to the Law of the Jews, after Our Lord Jesus Christ has come. I * am the Lord who expounds the truth and indicates wisdom. Gather yourselves; come and take counsel together, you who have been saved from among the peoples. Moreover he says: I am indeed the Lord. There is no other Just One and (no other) Life-giver than me; there is (no one) like me. Turn toward me and you will live, you who (come) from the ends of the earth. I am the Lord and there is no other

ኩ ፡ በርእስየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይሚጥ ፣ ቃልየ ፡ ዘወፅአ ፡ እምአሩየ ፣ እስመ ፣ ሊተ ፣ ይሰግ ድ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ብርክ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ልሳን ፡ ይገኒ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይብል ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወ ጽድቅ ፣፤ ኀቤሁ ፡= ወይመጽኡ ፣ ወይትሐፈሩ ፡ ዥሎሙ ፡ እለ ፣ ይርሕቁ ፡ እምእግዚአ ብሔር =፡ ወሙሴኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ይቤ ፡፡ ወይረድ ፣ ከመ ፡ ጠል ፡ ነቢብየ ፡=፡ ወከመ ፡ ዝ ናም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገራህት ።፡፡ ወከመ ፡ ጊሜ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፀምር ፡ ይቤ ፣፡ ነቢይ ፡።፡ ኢትፍራ ህ ፤ አስመ ፡ ኢትትኅፈር ፣ ወኢትደንግፅ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘተሰባእከ ፡=፡ ይቤ ፣ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነ ቢይ ፡=፡ ሕያው ፡ አነ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡=፡
than me. I have sworn it by myself, so as not to withdraw my word which has gone out of my mouth: Behold every knee will bow before me and every tongue will supplicate the Lord and will say to him glory and justice. All those who have turned away from the Lord will come and will be confounded. The prophet Moses says: * May my word descend like the dew, like the rain upon a field and like the snow upon the wool. The prophet says: Do not fear, for you will not be confounded, and do not be terrified that you have been treated like a man. The prophet Isaiah says: I am living, says the Lord.
ይቤ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፣ ነቢይ = ወያድኅኖሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይእተ ፡ አሚረ ፡፣ከመ፣ አባግዐ ፣ ሕዝቡ ።፡ እስመ ፣ እብን ፡ ቅድስት ፣ ያንኩረዙራ ፣ ውስት ፡ ምድር ።፡ እሉ፣ እሙንቱ ፣ አርዳኢሁ! ፡ ለአግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፣፡ ክርስቶስ ፡፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነ ቢይ ፡፡ ሰማያት ፣፡ ይነግራ ፣ ስብሐተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ወግብረ ፣ እደዊሁ ፡ ያየድዓ ፡ ሰማያት = ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ።= ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡= ወእስከ ፡ አጽ
The prophet Zechariah says: The Lord will save them that day, like the sheep of his people. Indeed, holy stones will be rolled upon the earth. These are the disciples of Our Lord Jesus Christ. As the prophet David says: The heavens recount the glory of the Lord; the heavens state the work of his hands. Furthermore he says: Into all the earth has gone their word; until the ends of the world has reached their * word.
ናፈ ፡ ዓለም ፣ በጽሐ ፡ ነቢቦሙ ፡፡ ይቤ ፤ ኢሳይያስ ፤ ነቢይ ፡=፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላኪየ ፣ ኢይትሄየየኒ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ መኩንን ፡ አስተርአየ ፡፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክነ ፡፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡
The prophet Isaiah says: The Lord, my God, will not abandon me; the Lord, (my) judge, has appeared. The Lord is our God; the Lord is our king; he himself the Lord will save us. Furthermore he says: Behold he

እግዚአብሔር ፡፡ ወካዕበ ፣ ይቤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሐዲሥ ፡ ስርዐት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡= ስምዑ ኒ' ፡ ሕዝብየ ፡ ወአጽምኡኒ ፡ ተግሣጽየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ይወጽእ ፡ እምኔየ ፣ ወፍት ሕየኒ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ትመጽእ ፡ ጽድቅየ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወትበጽሕ ፡ መድኀኒትየ ፡ ወይ ትዌከሉ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ በመዝራአትየ ።፡ ኪያየ ፡ ይሴፈዉ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ወደሰያት ፡ ወይት ዌከሉ ፡ በመዝራዕትየ ፡፡
He says again concerning the New Testament, that is to say, the Holy Gospel: Listen to me, my people, and lend an ear to my exhortation! Indeed, the Law will go out from me and my rule will be the light of the peoples. My justice will come rapidly and my salvation will arrive (quickly). The peoples will trust in my arm; * the peoples and the islands will hope in me and will trust in my arm.

Again (he says) concerning Christ: That day, the Lord-God will appear to the peoples in justice and uprightness. Every being (endowed) with a soul will see the salvation of the Lord. Furthermore he says: (The Lord) said to me: A great (role) is (reserved) for you: (you must be called my servant, you must raise up the people of Jacob and you must bring back the dispersed Israelites). Behold I have established you as a light for the peoples, so that you may be (for them) the Savior until the ends of the earth. Thus says the Lord who saved you, the God of Israel: Proclaim him holy, he who will afflict his soul (and) whom the peoples will execrate because of the work of his divinity. I will establish a Testament of eternal justice for the faithful David. Behold I have set him as a witness for the peoples. Those who did not know you will invoke you; the peoples who did not know you * will put themselves under your protection because of the Lord-God, Holy One of Israel, for he himself has glorified you. Seek the Lord. As soon as you have found him, invoke him; as soon as you have approached him, (pray to him). Let the sinner abandon his way and the iniquitous man his project! Let him return to the Lord, for he will remit your many sins! Indeed, my design is not like your design and your way is not (like my way), says the Lord. The prophet Jeremiah says: My house will be called a house of prayer for all the peoples. The prophet Isaiah says: He who walks in justice, who speaks the truth, who hates the way of iniquity, who hates sin and deceit, who turns his hand away from bribery, who turns his ear away so as not to hear the condemnation of a soul, and veils his eye so as not to see oppression, that one will dwell in the high cave of a solid rock; food will be given to him as well as water with justice. You will see the king with (his) glory; your eyes will see a land extending far away; your soul will be anxious. Furthermore he says concerning baptism: The Lord says: I will make gush forth * rivers in the mountains; I will make gush forth (springs) in the midst of the desert; I will make a forest grow in the desert; I will make water flow in the land of thirst. Moreover he says: Let those who are thirsty come to drink water.

63. Arrival of the Jew Justus. He blames Jacob and the newly baptized. - Five days later, came to them a Jew from the East, called Justus, of the family of Isaac. (Isaac) recounted to him everything that had happened among * the Jews. Justus said to him: "It is not good (for you) to have been baptized, for the time of holy baptism has not arrived. Furthermore, the Christ has not come." Isaac answered and said to him: "Behold formerly we remained in great sadness, until there came to us a Jew from the city of Constantinople called Jacob. He taught us and revealed to us the faith and the truth. Behold for me it seems that this man speaks by the Holy Spirit, for he has demonstrated to us by the Holy Scriptures that Our Lord Jesus Christ has come."

While (Justus and Isaac) were conversing about this, brothers arrived

ለ፤'፣ ዮስጦስ = ተሰጥወ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አነ ፡ አአምሮ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፣ፈያታ ዊ ፡ ፅልው ፡ "ወእኩይ ፣ ዘተቃረኖሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ በኅቤ ሆሙ ፡ብዙኀ ፡ ሕማመ ፡ ወምንዳቤ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ ፉቃ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሶበሰ ፡ ረከብክዎ ፡ እምከሠትኩ ፡ ሐሰቶ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይትቃረኖ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ። ወናሁ ፣ አን ትሙሰ ፣፡ ጌገይክሙ ፡ ዘተጠመቅሙ" =፡ ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ እለ ፣ ተጠምቁ ፡ አኀው ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ በኀበ ፣ ዮስጦስ ፡ ተሀው ኩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡=፡ ወይቤ ፡ 8እምኔሆሙ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ለነ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፣፡ ጌጋዮ ፡ ወሐ ሰቶ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፤ እምይእዜሰ ፡ ኢነሐዩ ፡ በሕገ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሀከከ ፡ ወጋእዘ ፡ ማዕከሎሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ሐዘን =
to Isaac and spoke with Justus. Justus answered and said to them: "I, I know Jacob; he is a brigand, an impious * and a wicked man, he who was relentless against the Christians. These have suffered from him a great torment and a (great) persecution, in the days of King Phocas. If I met him, I would unveil his lie (and I would reproach him) for having been relentless against the living Lord. Behold you yourselves have sinned, you who have been baptized." The brothers who were baptized, hearing these words from Justus, were extremely troubled. One of them said: "If Justus unveils to us the sin and the lie of Jacob, from now on we will no longer live by the Law of the Christians." There was tumult and quarrels among them as well as great sadness.
ወበሳኒታ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ በከመ ፡ ያለምድ ፡ኅበ ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡ ወርእዮሙ ፡፤ እንዘ ፡ ትኩዛን ፣ እሙንቱ ፣ ወያንጐረጉሩ ።፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ናሁ ፡ እሬአ[የ]8ሙ ፤ ተሐዝኑ ፤ ወትቴክዙ ። ወአልቦ ፤ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘተሠጥዎ ፡ ቃ ለ = ወአ[ም]ገዝ ፤ ኅደጎሙ፤" ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ወኀለፈ ፡ እምሀየ ፡፡ ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ነስሑ ፡ በ እንተ ፣ ዘኢአጽሐፉ ፣ ዘተባሐሉ ፣ በበ[ይ]ናቲሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግርዎ ፣ ለዮስጦስ ፡፡ አውሥአ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኢትሕዝኑ ፡ ኦአኀው ፡ናሁ ፡ አዘዝክዎ" ፣ ለወ
64. Jacob learns of the arrival of the Jew Justus. - The next day, Jacob came, as he was accustomed, to the place where they were meeting. He saw them sad and murmuring. He said to them: * "Behold I see you sorrowful and sad." No one among them answered him a word. Then Jacob left them and departed from there. As for them, they repented of not having had written what they had discussed among themselves, in order to expose it to Justus. Joseph answered and said to them: "Do not be sorrowful, O brothers. Behold


[p. 28] {#po.13.p28}
ልድየ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ይንበር ፡ ድኅረ ፡ ፕኅት ፡ ወይጽሐፍ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘተናገርክሙ ፡ በበይ ናቲክሙ ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ዘአዘዝክዎ ፡፡ ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በኅበ ፣፡ዬዮ ሴፍ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ፈድፋደ፣
I have ordered my son Simeon to sit behind the door and to write everything that you discussed among yourselves. He himself has done everything that I ordered him." Having learned this from Joseph, they rejoiced extremely.
ወካዕበ ፣ ተጋብኡ ፡ ዳግመ ፡=፡ ወእምዝ ፣ መጽአ ፣ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ያዕቆብ ፣ ወይቤሎ ሙ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አሬእየክሙ ፣ ኦአኀው ፡ ተሐዝኑ ፡ ወትቴክዙ =፡ ተሠጥወ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወ ይቤሎ ፡ ርቱዕ ፡ ለነ ፤ ኦአኀው ፡ ንሕዝን ፣፤ "”ወንብኪ ፡፣ እስመ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤነ ፡ ብእ ሲ ፡ ዘእምዘመድየ' ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ምሁረ ፡ ኦሪት =፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ አነሂ ፣ አአምሮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡፣ ብእሲ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ እሩሁ ፣ አብ ርሃም ፣ ወአባሆሙ ፣ ሰሙኤል ፡ ጥዩቀ ፡ እሉ ፣ ምሁራነ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ ወአነ ሂ ፡ በኀበ ፣ አቡሆሙ ፡፣ ተምህርኩ ፣ ኦሪተ ፡ ወነቢያተ = ወይእዜኒ ፡ አእስእለክሙ ፡ ታምጽእዖ ፡ ይንግረነ ፡ ዘመሀሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡=፡ ይቤ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ እመሰ ፡ አባሕከኒ" ፡ አመጽኦ ፡ አነ ፡፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አባሕኩከ ፡ ታምጽኦ ፡ ኅቤነ ፡=፡
They gathered together again for a second time. Then Jacob came to them and said to them: "Behold, I see you, O brothers, sorrowful and sad." Isaac answered and said to him: "It is right for us, O brothers, that we should be sorrowful * and that we should weep. Indeed, a man of my family, called Justus, has come to us. He himself knows (thoroughly) the..." Jacob said to them: "I too know this man, as well as his brother Abraham and their father Samuel. Certainly they know (thoroughly) the Law and the prophets. I myself learned the Law and the prophets from their father. For the moment I ask you to have him come, so that he may explain to us what the Lord has taught him." Isaac said: "If you permit me (to do so), I will bring him myself." He said to him: "Behold, I permit you to bring him to us."
ወእምዝ ፣ ሖረ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፣ ኅበ ፡ ዮስጥስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽኦ ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የ ሐሥሥ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ በዘ ፡ ያስተኀፍሮሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ተጠምቁ ፡ አይሁድ ፡፡
65. Isaac decides that Justus should debate with Jacob. - Then Isaac went to Justus, in order to bring him. He found him scrutinizing the Scriptures by means of which (he could) confound the Jews who had been baptized.


[p. 29] {#po.13.p29}
ይቤሎ ፤ ይስሐቅ ፤ ያፅቆብሰ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ይርአይከ ፡ ወየ”አምሕክ ፡፤ ወይበልከ ፡ ባ ሐ ይቤ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይፈቆቅድ ፡ እርአይ ፡ ገጾ ፣ ወእመሂ ፡ ነጸርክዎ ፡ እትነሣ እ ፤ ወአሠርር ላዕሌሁ ፣ ወባሕቱ ፡፣ እፈርህ ፣ እምከመ ፡ ሳምዑ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ያመን ድቡኒ' ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡፡ ወዕበሰ ፣ ሀለውኩ ፡ በዐካ ፡ ሀገር ፡ አምከሠትኩ ፡ ሎቱ ፣፡ እምኦሪ ት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢገመጽአ" ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወኢቀርቡ ፡ ምጽአቶ ፡፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ይስሐ ቅ ፡ ነዓ ፣ ንትመሐል ፡ በበይናቲነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢናሰምእ ፡ ነገረነ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ዝዘእንበ ለ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ንንበር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኅቡእ ፡ መካን ።፡፡ ወከማሁ ፡ ያፅቆብኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢይፈቅድ ፡ ይስምፁ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡፣ ዘንተ ፡ ምስጢረ ፡ ነገር ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ተመሐ
Isaac said to him: "Jacob wants to see you, * to greet you and to say hello to you." Justus said: "As for me, I do not want to see his face. If I catch sight of him, I will get up and leap upon him. But I fear that the Christians, as soon as they learn of it, will persecute me extremely. If I were in the city of Acre, I would demonstrate to him by the Law and the prophets that Christ has not come and that his coming is not near." Isaac said to him: "Come. We have sworn an oath among ourselves not to let the Christians hear our exposition, but on the contrary to sit in a hidden place. Likewise Jacob, he too does not want the Christians to hear this mysterious exposition. That is why we have sworn an oath among ourselves."
ልነ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡፤ ናሁ ፡ አነ ፤ አሌብወክሙ ፡፣ ከመ ፣ ጌገይክሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ተጠመቅሙ ፡ወ ያዕቆብኒ ፡ ጌገየ ፡ ዘአስሐተክሙ"፣ ተመሲሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ” ሰይጣን ፡=፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ኀብሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡ በሣኒታ ፣ ወኀለፉ ፡ እምሕየ ፡= ወዮስጦስሰ ፣ ተግሀ ፣ ኩላ ፣ ሌሊተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐሥሥ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ኦሪተ ፡ ወነ ቢያተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተኅፍሮሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡=፡
Justus (said): "Behold, I myself will make you understand that you have sinned, you who have been baptized. Jacob also has sinned, he who led you astray, after having become like * Satan." Then they agreed to meet the next day and they departed from there. Justus, for his part, stayed awake all night, scrutinizing the Scriptures, the Law and the prophets, in order to confound the Jews who had become Christians.


[p. 30] {#po.13.p30}

### FOURTH ASSEMBLY
ራብእ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ወበሳኒታ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፤ ወምስሌሁ ፡ አኀው ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ኀ በ ፡ይትጋብኡ ፣፡ኅቡረ፡ ' ወረከብዎ ፡ ለዮስጦስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ክርታስ ።፡ ወዕበ ፣ ርእዮ ፣ ያዕቆብ ፣ ለዮስጦስ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ባሐ ፡ እጉነ ፣ ወመምህሀርነ፡፣ ዮስጦስ ፡ ገብር ፡ ቴር ፡፡ ወእምዝ ፣ ይቤሎ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ምንት ፡ አስሐትኮሙ ፡ ለአርዳ እየ ፡ ወአውፃአእኮሙ ፡ እምፍኖተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣ ወሜጥኮሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዕልወት ፡=፡ አውሥአ፤ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትትመንዓዕ ፡ ኦዮስጦስ ፡=፡ አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ መፍትው ፡ ሊተ ፡ እትመዓዕ ፡ ወእትቄጣእ ፡ እስመ ፣ አንተ ሰ ፡ አብድ ፡ ዕልው ፤ ኃጥእ ፡ ወአባሲ ፣ እምዙሎሙ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወትሬሲ ፡ ርእሰከ ፡
66. Interview between Jacob and Justus. - The next day, Jacob came as well as the brothers to the place where they were meeting together. They found Justus sitting with Isaac; in their hands was a volume. When Jacob saw Justus, he said to him: "Greetings, our brother and our teacher Justus, good servant (of the Lord)!" Then Justus said to Jacob: "Why have you led my disciples astray, have you led them out of the path of truth and have you turned them into apostasy?" * Jacob answered and said to him: "Do not be angry, O Justus." (Justus) answered and said to him: "I must be angry and indignant, for you are more foolish, impious, sinful and criminal than all the Jews. You have set yourself up as a teacher and you have played the wise man for an evil work."

of Isaac, of Jacob, of Moses and of all the prophets who have served the Lord in justice, knows that I myself was not baptized of my own will, (but) that I became a Christian against * my will. That is why I lived in great sorrow and (in great) torment. Then I began to pray and to beseech the merciful Lord, day and night, with many tears and weeping, so that he might instruct me (and) that I might know the path of truth. Then I began to scrutinize the Holy Scriptures. Behold, the Lord revealed to me knowledge, intelligence and wisdom. Then I recognized and I was certain that he concerning whom the Law and the prophets had announced and (he) whose coming they had preached was the Good Shepherd, that is to say, Christ who was born of Mary, of the Holy Virgin in Bethlehem of Judah, in the days of King Augustus Caesar and was crucified in the days of Pontius Pilate. As for him who says that he is not the Christ * and (who) does not believe in him, let him be anathema!"

68. Invectives of Justus. - Justus answered and said to Jacob: "O senseless one, are you stronger than I in the knowledge of the Law and the prophets? Do you not know then that it is my father the teacher Samuel who taught you the Scriptures of the Lord?"

Jacob said to him: "Far from you (such a thought)! I am not like you; I am not wiser than you; I do not have more knowledge (than you). However, may the Holy Spirit who spoke through the mouth of the prophets refute you himself and anathematize him who does not believe in Christ!" Immediately Justus rose in great indignation and (in great) anger; he threw a band around Jacob's neck, strangled him and said to him: "The Lord knows that, if you do not demonstrate to me and do not explain to me through the Holy Scriptures of the prophets how (the coming of Christ took place), behold, I myself * will kill you today."

Then Isaac and Theodore rose, (interposing themselves) between them, and said (to Justus): "It is not fitting for teachers to act thus. Behold, you yourself do not have the right on your side." Furthermore, Joseph said to Justus: "Such an (act) is not the deed of wise men, but rather one must make peace between you in patience and humility." Then he said to him: "Behold, I formerly ordered my son Simeon to write down all the words that he heard from Jacob. Now therefore I am sending for this writing, so that you may read and understand everything that we have discussed among ourselves."

69. Justus asks for a week to prepare for the controversy. - Justus said to him: "Grant me eight days, so that I may scrutinize the Scriptures, and I will demonstrate to you that Christ has not come and furthermore that (the time) of holy baptism has not arrived." As for them, * they said to him: "Assuredly, we will listen to everything that you will tell us." (Then) they returned to their homes.

### FIFTH ASSEMBLY

Eight days later, they met. There was a great quarrel between them and much tumult. Charity was far from them and they did not agree. Then they separated from one another.

70. Controversy with Justus. - Behold, they all met. Justus answered and said to them: "As for you, you have sinned, you who have been baptized and have gone into the path of the apostate Jacob. Behold, I myself (am going) to explain to you how you have sinned."

71. God has rejected his people. - Jacob answered him and said to him: "Understand, O my brother Justus, what the Lord said to the prophet Hosea: Go, marry a prostitute woman and (have) children of a prostitute, for truly * the earth prostitutes itself far from the Lord. He went and married Gomer, daughter of Diblaim. She conceived and bore him a son. The Lord said to him: He shall be called by the name of Jezreel, for a little (time) yet and I will avenge the blood of Jezreel upon the house of Judah; I will abolish the royalty of the house of Israel. (When) that day arrives, I will break the bow of Israel in the valleys of Jezreel. She conceived again, a second time, and bore a daughter. The Lord (said to him): She shall be called "She-who-is-not-pitied" for I will no longer have pity again on the house of Israel, but on the contrary I will destroy them. Furthermore (Hosea) said: She conceived again and bore a son. (The Lord) said to him: He shall be called by the name of "Not-my-people" for you yourselves will no longer be my people and I neither will I be your God * Justus said: "Truly it is so written (in Hosea)."
ይቤ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ እሙነ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡፡ ትተሠጥወ፤" ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሆሴዕ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በእንተ ፣፡ አሕዛብ
72. Prediction of the conversion of the peoples and of the rejection of the Old Law. - Jacob answered and said to him: "Thus says the prophet Hosea concerning the peoples

who formerly remained without believing in the Lord, (but who) afterwards recognized him and believed in him: It will happen that in the land where it was said to them: "You are not my people", they will be called the sons of the living Lord. Then the children of Judah and the children of Israel will be gathered together and a prince will be set over them (who) will be raised from the earth, for great will be the day of Jezreel. These are the peoples who have believed in Christ. One is their kingdom; one is their prince, that is to say, Christ. As for (the word) that the prophet said: He will be raised from the earth, (it means) that, as soon as Christ had come, * no one served the (false) gods on earth anymore. But on the contrary, they worshipped the heavenly God and relied upon him, for great was the day of Jezreel, (according to) what the prophet says. It is therefore the day of the coming of Christ which made his light rise upon all (the peoples), led them into the path of truth, turned them away from error (to convert them) to knowledge, abolished the worship of (false) gods and revealed faith in the Lord.
አሚነ ፤ በእግዚአብሔር ፡=፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡፣ በእንተ ፡ እለ፣ ተማከሩ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ክርስቶስ ። እስመ ፣ ይእቲሰ፣ ኢኮነ ት ፡ ብአሲትየ ፡ ወአነሂ ፡ ኢኮንኩ ፡ ምታ ።፡ ወናሁ ፡ እስዕር ፣ ዝሙታ ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ገጽየ ፡ ወማሕዛኒሃ ፡ አማዕከለ ፡ አጥባቲሃ" ፡= ወእቀፍጻ ፣፡ ፅራቃ ፣ ወአሬስያ ፡ ከመ ፡
Furthermore he says concerning those who plotted against Christ: Indeed, she is not my wife and I neither am I her husband. Behold, I will annihilate her debauchery from before my face and her lovers from between her breasts. I will uncover her naked; I will make her as in the days of her childhood; I will make her like

መዋዕለ ፡ ንእሳ ፡ ወአእገብራ ፣ ከመ ፡ በድው ፡ ወእሬስያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ምድር ፡ ዘአል"ቦ ፣ ማየ ፡ ወአቀትላ ፡ በጽምአ ፡ ወኢይሣሀሎሙ ፡፣ እንክ ፡ ለደቂቃ ፡ አስመ ፡ ትውልደ ፡ ዘ ማ ፡እሙንቱ'፣እስመ ፡ዘመወት"፣እሞሙ ፡ ወአስተኀፈረቶሙ'! ፡ ልደቶሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ አሐውር ፡ እትልዎሙ ፡ ለማሕዛንየ ፡ እለ ፡ ይሁቡኒ ፡ ስቴየ ፡ ወመብልፅዕየ ፡ ወይ ኤርዙኒ አልባስየ ፣ ወመዋጥሕየ' ፣ ወይሁቡኒ ፡ ቅብአየ ፡ ወዙሎ ፡ ተድላየ ፍ በእንተ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ናሁ ፡ አነ ፣ አሐጽር ፡ ፍኖታ ፡ በሦክ ፡ ወእፌጽማ ፡ ፍ ኖታ ፡ ወተኀጥእ ፡ እንከ ፡ ምዕራፈ ፡ ወትዴግኖሙ ፡ እንከ ፣ ለማኅፈሪሃ ፡ ወኢት[አ]ሱ ዞሙ ፡ ወተኀሦሙ ፡ ወኢትረክቦሙ ፡ ወትብል ፡ አሐውር ፡ እንከ ፡፣ ኅበ ፡ ምትየ ፡ ዘት ካት፣፣ ይጌይሰኒ፣ ቀዲሙ ፡ እምይእዜ ፡ ወይእቲሰ ፡ ኢያአመረት ፡ ከመ ፡ አነ ፡ውእቱ"፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ "ዘወሀብክዋ ፡ እክለ ፡ ወወይነ ፡ ወቅብአ ፡ ወአብዛኅኩ"፣ ላቲ፣ ወርቀ ፡= ወይእቲሰ ፡ ወርቀ ፣ ወብሩረ ፡ ገብረት ፡ ለበዓል ፡ በእንተ ፣ ዝንቱ ፣ እትመየጥ ፡ ወአስ ተጋብእ ፡ እክልየ ፣ ዘበጊዜሁ ፡ ወወይንየ ፡ ዘበበ ፡ ዕለቱ ፡ ወእሰልባ ፣ አልባስየ ፡ ወመ ዋጥሕየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትክድን ፣ ኅፍረታ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እከስታ ፣ ኅፍረታ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ማሕዛኒሃ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘያድኅና ፡ እምእዴየ ፡ ወእመይጥ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ በዓላቲሃ ፡ ወፍሥሓሃ፣
a desert; I will make her like a land without * water; I will kill her by thirst and I will no longer have pity on her children, for they are the generation of a prostitute. Indeed, their mother has prostituted herself and has dishonored their birth, since she said: I am going to follow my lovers who will give me my drink and my food, will provide me my clothes and my linens and will give me my oil and all my delights. That is why the Lord says: Behold, I myself will surround her path with thorns and I will close her path. She will no longer have a place of rest; she will therefore pursue her acquaintances and will not catch them; she will seek them and will not find them. She will say: I will therefore go to my former husband, (for my situation) of old was preferable for me to (that of) now. As for her, she did not recognize that it was I the Lord * who gave her the food, the wine and the oil, and lavished gold upon her. But she, she used the gold and the silver (in the service of) Baal. That is why I will take back and I will gather my food in its time and my wine in its day; I will strip her of my clothes and my linens, so that she does not cover her pudenda. Behold, now I will uncover her pudenda before her lovers. No one will deliver her from my hand. I will abolish all her feasts, her rejoicings,

her new moons, her sabbaths and all her customs. I will make her vines, her fig trees and everything of which she said: It is my wage (of a prostitute) that my lovers gave me, disappear. I will make (her vines and her fig trees) become an example. The beasts of the field, the birds of the sky and the serpents will eat (them). I will avenge myself on her, (because) she * sacrificed to the Baals, adorned herself with her earrings and her necklaces, was not ashamed, followed her lovers and forgot me, says the living Lord. Again Jacob said: "O Justus, behold what the prophet expressed concerning the society of the Jews who do not believe in Christ. That is why (the Lord) said: I will make their feasts, their rejoicings, their new moons, their sabbaths and all their customs cease. What is there then more exact than this word? Indeed, behold it demonstrates that the sabbath, the feasts and the sacrifice have been abolished.
ወበእንተዝ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ አለ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ትእ[ዛ፲]ዛቲሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከ መዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡፡ ይእተ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አራዊተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወምስለ ፡ አእዋፈ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ አራዊተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወእስእር ፡ ቀስተ ፣ ወኩናተ ፡ ወፀባኢተ ፣ እም ድር ፡ ወይነብሩ ፡ "ተአሚኖሙ ፡ ወአዐቅቦሙ ፡ እስከ ፣ ለዓለም ፡ በጽድቅ ፡ ወበርትፅ ፡ በሣህል ፡ ወበምሕረት ፡ ወያአምሩኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡፡ ኢትሰምፅ
That is why (there will be) a new people who will accomplish the orders of the Lord. Thus says the prophet: That day, (I will make for them a covenant) with the beasts of the field, with the birds of the sky and with the serpents; I will make the bow, the sword and the armies disappear from the earth. They will remain * in security. I will keep them forever in justice, in uprightness, in clemency and in mercy. They will know that it is I the Lord. Do you not hear,

O my brother Justus, what the prophet says: They will know me? It is the people who recognized Christ and believed in him. Furthermore he says: It will happen that day, says the Lord, that I will answer the sky; the sky will answer the earth; the earth will answer the grain, the wine and the oil; they too will answer Jezreel. I will sow for myself in the earth; I will love her who was (no longer) my friend and I will say: "My people" to him who was (no longer) my people; they too, they will call me their God. That means (that) it is the new people who have been baptized in the holy Church * and have believed in the orthodox faith. Furthermore the prophet says: There will no longer be famine again on earth for those who will listen to the word of the Lord. Indeed, the knowledge of the Lord will be complete upon all, like the immense water that covers the sea. There will stand, that day, the shoot of Jesse who will be constituted the prince of the peoples; the peoples will have confidence in him; his own place of rest will become glorious. Listen, my brother Justus, to this evident word. (It is true) that the Lord ordered the children of Israel not to be baptized, except only in the temple, and (said to them) that he would not accept their sacrifices except in (the temple. But) behold, all that has ceased. Indeed, the prophets have preached that the faith would arrive with the judgment of the world. The prophet Hosea says concerning the peoples * who


[p. 39] {#po.13.p39}

have converted to the Lord: They will seek the Lord, their God, and David, their king. They will remember the Lord and (his) benefits for the later days.
ወካዕበ ፣ ይቤ ፡= ወእሰውጥ ፡ ወአሐውር ፡ እስከ ፡ አሜ ፡ ይጠፍኡ ፡ ወየኅሠ ፡ ገ ጽየ ፡ በምንዳቤሆሙ ፡ ወይገይሱ ፡ ኅቤየ ፣ ወይብሉ ፡ ንሖር ፡ ንትመየጥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ አምላክነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ቀሰፈነ ፡ ወይፌውስ ፡ ለነ ፡ ወአቀሰለነ ፡ ወይሰ ርየነ ፡ ወያሐይወነ ፡ በሰኑይ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወአሜ ፡ ሣልስት ፡ ፅለት ፡ ንትነሣእ ፡ ወነሐዩ ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡መኑ ፡ እንከ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ወይሔልዮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፣ ወይሌብዎ ፡ ወያአምሮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ርቱዓት' ፡ እማንቱ ፣ ፍናዊሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወካ ጽቃን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ የሐውርዎን ፡ ወኃጥኣንሰ ፡ ይስእንዎን ፡፡
Furthermore he says: I will return and I will go away, until they perish and seek my face in their torment. They will come in the morning to me and will say of me: Come, let us turn to the Lord, our God, for he himself has scourged us, (but) he will heal us; he has wounded us, (but) he will heal us. He will make us live again the second day; the third day we will rise and we will live before him. Furthermore he says: Who then will be wise, will meditate on this, will understand and will know this? Indeed, the ways * of the Lord are straight; the just, they, go therein, (but) the sinners cannot (go therein).
ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሀየ ፣ ጸላእክዎሙ ፡፣ በእንተ ፡ እከዮሙ ፣ ወእሰድይሙ" ፡ እምአብያቲሆሙ ፡ ወኢይርደ]ገግም ፡ እንከ ፡ ተሣህሎቶሙ ፡= መላእክቲሆሙኒ ፡ ዐላው ያን ደወየ ፡ ኤፍሬም ፡ ወየብሳ ፡ ሥረዊሁ ፣ ወኢይፈሪ ፡ እንከ ፡=፡ እመኒ ፡ ወለዱ ፡ እ ቀትል ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ክርሦሙ ፡ መፍትው ፡ ገደፎሙ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ አበዩ ፡ ሰሚ ዖቶ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ እምውስቴቶሙ ፡ መታሕላነ" ፡ በውስተ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡=፡ ወክፅበ። ይቤ ፡ እዌሩ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ መሥገርትየ ፡ ከመ ፡ አፅ[ዋ]ፈ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወአውረዶሙ ፡
73. Prediction of the unbelief of the Jews and of the sufferings of Christ. - Again he says: It is there that I hated them because of their malice. I will drive them from their houses and I will not begin again to be clement toward them. Their princes also are rebels. Ephraim is sick; his roots have become dry; he will no longer bear fruit. Even if they give birth, I will kill the children of their womb. (As) is fitting, the Lord has repudiated them, because they refused to listen to him; they will become, (for the most part) among them, wanderers among the peoples. Furthermore he says: I will cast my net over them, like (over)


[p. 40] {#po.13.p40}

the birds of the sky, and it will make them descend. I will punish them in their ears by a torment. Woe to them, because * they have traveled (far) from me! They have been terrified, because they have sinned against me, but I, I have saved them. As for them, they have outraged me by lies and they do not cry out to me with all their heart. Furthermore he says: Against me they have plotted evil and they have turned in vain; they have become like a bent bow; their princes will fall by the sword because of the foolishness of their tongue. Again he says: Israel has been swallowed up; it has become like a vile vessel among the peoples. The prophet says: Behold, I will order that they be dispersed among the peoples. Like the flour that falls to the ground, so will all the sinners of (my) people fall. They will fall by the sword, those who say: We will not encounter misfortune; affliction will not come upon us. * Have you seen, my brothers, what the prophet said concerning the peoples who will not believe in Christ? (He said) how they will be dispersed among the peoples, as soon as Christ has come; how the royalty will be taken away from the Jews; and how their priesthood and their sacrifice will be abolished. As for them, they have become (beings) repudiated, rejected and dispersed among all the peoples until today.


[p. 41] {#po.13.p41}

The prophet Isaiah says concerning the people who crucified Christ and killed him: I have turned my face from you, so as not to be clement toward you. Indeed, blood has filled your hands and your fingers; your lips have uttered iniquity; your tongue has enunciated sin; you have expressed (things) useless for you; you have conceived affliction and * given birth to iniquity. Furthermore he says: Indeed, (their work) is a work of iniquity; their feet run for sin; they hasten to shed blood; they plot murder; ignominy and calamity are on their path. Indeed, their path is the rough path; they do not know the path of peace; there is no justice in their mouth, for the path in which they go is tortuous. That is why justice is far from them. The prophet David says: Their feet are swift in all evil; they run to shed just blood; ignominy and calamity are on their path; they do not know the path of peace; the fear of the Lord is not before their eyes; * all the authors of iniquity do not know (it). The prophet Isaiah says: That is why justice is far from them; uprightness does not reach them; their light has become for them darkness; they grope at the walls, like blind men who have no eyes; they fall in the day, as if they were in the middle of the night; they are tormented, as if they were dying; they go

74. Christ has come. - If this (Jesus) were not the Christ, - (he who) rose, (as) light, (coming) from Mary, the Holy Virgin; (he) concerning whom the prophets announced; (he who) appeared to men, - the children of Israel would remain in royalty, in greatness and glory, as they were formerly; furthermore * all the peoples would not believe in him. But we know and we are certain that he is the true Christ concerning whom the prophets prophesied. Since he was crucified until today, behold, we remain in torment, affliction, sadness and captivity. The Lord has become irritated against us with a great anger that exceeds (that of) the captivity of Babylon, for in the captivity of Babylon, at the end of seventy years, the Lord had pity on the people (of the Israelites), delivered them and made them return to their land. Behold, what our father Jacob said has been accomplished: The dominion will not disappear from Judah (nor) from his members, until he meets Him who is reserved for him and is the hope of the peoples. When Christ had come, the peoples believed in him and the whole world obeyed him. Behold, the prophecies of the prophets

have been accomplished: the Law has been abolished; the royalty has been taken away from us; he made us leave our country and he dispersed us in all the earth until today."

75. Justus does not admit that Christ has come. - Justus said: * "Behold, I tell you, O Jacob: As for us, we await the coming of Christ, Savior of the whole world. Now to me, it seems (to me) that Christ has not yet come, for it is written in the Holy Scriptures that Christ will come suddenly, without being noticed. It is said concerning the Son of the king, that is to say (of Christ): He will descend, like the rain upon the field"

76. Exposition of the prophecy of Daniel. The two descents of Christ. - Jacob answered and said: "O my brother Justus, did I not make you swear, by the God of Abraham, of Isaac, and of Jacob, that you would explain to me that about which I would question you?" He said to him: "Assuredly, I will explain it to you." Jacob said: "Teach me for what (time) the prophet Daniel foretold the coming
ተሠጥወ፤" ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፳ወ፪ ፡ ሱባዔ ፡ ለሐኒጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅ ደስ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘበ ፡ አማን ፡።፡ ወእምድኅሬሁ" ፡ ሐሳዊ ፡ መሲሕ ፡ፍ፡ ወእም ድኅረ ፡ ሐሳዊ ፡ መሲሕ ፡ ትመጽእ ፡ ዓባይ ፡ ዕለተ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ወልደ ፣ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡=፡
of Christ." Justus answered and said to him: "Sixty-nine weeks after the reconstruction of the temple, the true Christ will come. After him will come the false Messiah. After the false Messiah will come the great day of the Lord, that is to say, the day of the Son of Man, as the prophet Daniel said."

መጽእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ፪ጊዜ ፡ ወሚመ ፡ አልቦ ። ዮስጦስ ፡ እወ ፡ እጉየ፣ ሕያው ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢዮኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡፡ ወይእተ ፡ አሚረ ፡ አእክዑ ፡ እምነ፣ መንፈስየ ፡ ወአገብር ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ በሰማይ ፡ በላዕሉ ፣፡ ወበምድር ፡ በታሕቱ ፡ ደም ፡ እሳ ት ፡ ወተይ፣ ጢስ ፡ ጸሓይኒ ፡ ይጸልም ፡ ወወርኅኒ ፡ ደመ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ትብ ጻሕ ፡ ዕለተ ፣ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ዓባይ ፡ እንተ ፣ ታስተርኢ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘጸውአ ፡ ስመ ፡
Christ will come into the world twice, yes or no?" Justus said to him: "Yes, my brother, the Lord is living; the prophet Joel says: On that day, I will pour out my Spirit and I will perform miracles in the heaven above and on the earth below: blood, fire, sulfur, smoke; the sun will be darkened and the moon will turn to blood, before the great day of the Lord comes, which will appear. Everyone who calls upon the name of the Lord will be saved."

Jacob said: "It is true, O my brother, that the prophets Daniel and Joel said such things; I myself believe in these things." Again he questioned him and said: "The four great beasts that Daniel mentioned, is it indisputable, yes or no?" * Justus answered and said to him: "Yes, the four great beasts that the prophet Daniel mentioned are the four kingdoms of the world. Then the ten horns, that means the ten kings. Then the horn that is small, that is the false Messiah. Then the Son of Man will come upon the clouds of heaven, in order to judge the living and the dead. The dream that Nebuchadnezzar had and that the prophet Daniel interpreted is true. Indeed, he said: The head of pure gold is the kingdom of the Chaldeans; the chest and the arms of silver are the

77. Justus confesses his embarrassment before Jacob's argumentation. - Furthermore, Jacob said to him: * "Explain this to me, O my brother Justus: Have the sixty-nine weeks passed, has the Christ come, and has the vision of the prophets been sealed, yes or no?" Justus said: "Assuredly, all these things that were foretold have been accomplished, and the Christ has come at the end of the kingdom of the Romans." Jacob said to him: "Is the kingdom of the Romans powerful, yes or no?" Justus said to him: "Yes, it is powerful. Now, to me, it seems that it will remain forever. Behold, the Christ has come, for the kingdom of the Romans is the fourth beast." Jacob said to him: "Since the sixty-nine weeks have passed since the beginning of the kingdom of the Romans and the prophecies of the prophets are consumed, then indisputably the Christ has come. How do you yourself await the coming of the Christ and say that he has not yet come?" Justus answered and said: "The Lord is living, you speak the truth. But for me, * I do not dare to say that no prophet has come after (Jesus) came. Our Lord Jesus Christ who was born of

Mary, of the Holy Virgin, and was crucified in the days of Pontius Pilate, is therefore the true Christ who came for the salvation of the whole world. As the prophet Isaiah says: Behold, the Just One will come and his salvation will appear. Furthermore, the prophet says: If he has delayed, do not despair, for he will come quickly and he will not delay. Furthermore, he says: All flesh will see the salvation of the Lord. The prophet Zechariah says: Behold a man, called the Oriental; he will rise from below; he will purify the house of the Lord; he will himself wear his insignia; he will sit on his throne; the priest will judge at his right; the council will be between them both. Again * the prophet says: Rejoice, daughter of Zion; exult, daughter of Jerusalem. Behold, your king will come, just and meek, mounted on the foal of an ass. The prophet David says: From the mouth of children and infants you have prepared glorification.

78. Justus acknowledges that Jesus is the Christ. - Jacob answered and said to him: "It is true, my brother: it is written thus. The prophet Zechariah says: There will still come peoples who will dwell in many cities. many peoples and many nations will come with me, seeking the face of the Lord who dominates all, in Jerusalem, and imploring * the face of the Lord. Thus says the Lord who dominates all. I have seen, my brother, how the peoples have believed in Christ. Behold, the kingdom of the Romans has been powerful, that is to say, the meaning of the ten horns. Then will come the false Messiah, that is to say, the horn that is small; then will come the great day of the Lord; then the dead will rise from the tombs and will stand before the terrible tribunal. There are among them those who will enter into the kingdom of heaven; there are among them those who will be led to eternal punishment. Since it is so, what then are the Jews waiting for?"

Word and the Son of the Lord. As the prophet David says: The Lord sent his Word; he healed them and saved them from their death. Let everyone be cursed who does not believe that the Christ is God who became man! Indeed, the prophet says regarding those who do not believe in Christ and do not want to hear his Gospel: They have turned their face from him; they have made their ears deaf, so as not to hear; they have thickened their heart, so as not to understand what the Lord said in the Law regarding the Savior. The Lord sent his prophets. They did not listen to them. * He brought upon them the punishment, for he called them and they did not listen to him. Likewise, they themselves will call him and he will not listen to them, says the Lord who dominates all. The prophet Micah says: I myself will make them confounded and dispersed and I will remove them from my inheritance, for they have transgressed my Law and my ways."

79. The reprobation of the Jews. - Behold, all these things have come upon us, from the time the Christ was crucified until today. The Lord has become angry with us, for we have not believed in him. The prophet says regarding the people who crucified the Christ: Woe to those who plot iniquity and meditate evil on their bed! As soon as it is day, they accomplish their iniquity and they raise their hands against the Lord. Furthermore, he says: He will reprimand Israel, saying: My people, what have I done to you? In what have I wearied you? Tell me. Behold, I brought you out of the land of Egypt and I delivered you from the house of servitude. Jacob answered and said to him: "Do you acknowledge, my brother Justus, that the Christ is God who became man, and that the Jews regarded him as a weak man? Now, everything that the prophets prophesied regarding him has taken place and has come to pass."

The countries subject to the domination of Rome. - Justus said to Jacob: "How many countries are there over which the fourth beast has reigned in this world?" * Jacob said: "It has reigned * over 'Awfyânos [Ocean]; Bartânyâ [Britain]; 'As'itâr [Spain]; Barn'âyâ [France]; 'Antolyâ [Italy]; Le'arä [Hellas]; 'Abrâgiyâ [Thrace] with all its upper regions; Bitänyâ with its adjacent regions; 'Anotsokiyä [Antioch]; Soryâ [Syria] and all the provinces that are in it; the land of Fârès and Medyâm [Persia and Media]; Belänyâ and all the famous islands that are in the sea; 'Akentès; Fereqyâ [Africa] up to 'Awfyâs [Ocean], the sea that surrounds the entire world;

81. End of the controversy. - After having said these words, they set out and went away, that day, to their houses. Then * Jacob began to fast, to pray, and to ask the Lord to forgive him the sins he had committed formerly. Justus said to the brothers: "The Lord is living! Everything that Jacob has said is true and there is no lie in that, for it is stated by the Holy Spirit. Now I want you to bring me what you had written down formerly, that about which Jacob disputed with you." Then they brought him the book. Seeing it, Justus said: "My father Samuel was the master of Jacob, but the latter surpasses him in wisdom and is superior to him in knowledge. Formerly my father said that the Christ whom the Jews crucified is indisputably God and furthermore that no other God will come than him." Having heard this word, the brothers were astonished.


[p. 51] {#po.13.p51}

82. Fulfillment of the prophecy of Daniel. - Then Justus said to them: "Behold, I myself am going to tell you a surprising thing. There was in * the city of Soginä, in the days of King Moriq [Maurice], a man who lived near our dwelling; this man was a priest from among the princes of the Jews. I heard him, one day, in the process of saying: "Why do they rejoice at the death of King Moriq and the reign of Fougâ [Phocas]? Yet one will not rejoice at this subject. Behold, one sees calamities, from the time the kingdom of the fourth beast was weakened and destroyed, for there remains what will come before the ten horns of which the prophet Daniel speaks. Then will arise the horn that is small, that is to say, the fullness of fear. Then will take place the resurrection of the dead. Since it is so, understand that the Jews have stumbled and have abandoned the way of righteousness, for they did not believe in the Christ who came, before the kingdom of the fourth beast was destroyed." Then the Jews remained murmuring; they insulted this priest and there was a great quarrel between them. Behold, I myself heard this priest in the process of saying such a thing. But we, we remained mocking him." A few days later, they gathered again.

### SEVENTH ASSEMBLY

83. Jacob demonstrates that the Christ has come. - Justus said to Jacob: "I ask you to explain to me and to demonstrate to me by the word of the prophets the coming of the Christ, for the brothers want to scrutinize the Scriptures on this subject." Jacob said: "Yes, my brother. The Lord says by the mouth of the prophet Jeremiah: Do not trust in words of falsehood, for they will be of no use to you. Do not say: This house is the house of the Lord. If you make straight * your ways and your works, if you practice justice between men and between your fellows, if you do not oppress the widow, the orphan, and the stranger, if you do not shed pure blood in this house, if you do not go after foreign gods and do not follow them, I will make you yourselves dwell in the land that I gave to your fathers, from eternity to eternity. Again he says: If you listen to me and if you observe my commandment, you yourselves will enter into this house, your kings will sit on the throne of David, and you will be carried on the chariots of horses. But if you refuse to listen to my word and if you do not observe my commandment, behold, I swear to you myself, says the Lord, that I will demolish this house. Do you see, O my brother Justus, that,

since * the blood of Our Lord Jesus Christ was shed, the Lord has despised us and has scattered us in all countries; our temple has been destroyed; our Law and our Testament have been abolished. As the prophet Jeremiah says: Woe to me, for my soul perishes! The torment is great upon me. I say to my soul: Endure this affliction. Indeed, my sons will go out from my house and they will no longer return to me. No one will open my temple and will bring back my flock, for the shepherds have acted iniquitously and have not sought the face of the Lord. Furthermore, he says: Even if Moses and Samuel stood before my face, my soul would not be with them. I will not spare this people, but on the contrary, I will remove it from me and I will make it go out * outside. Furthermore, he says: Since you have abandoned me, says the Lord, and since you have turned behind other gods, I will raise my hand upon you, I will overturn you, and I will confound you. Again he says: I will not forgive them, says the Lord, but I will scatter them among the peoples and in all countries. Behold then, O my brother, that we have become scattered in all countries, for we have not listened to the word of Our Lord Jesus Christ. The prophet Micah says regarding the descent of the Christ: Behold, the Lord will go out from his house; he will descend and will dwell on the earth; the mountains will shake from beneath;

the hills will melt like wax before the face of the fire and like water that flows from the mountains. It is all this that will happen because * of the sin of Jacob and because of the crime of the house of Israel. The prophet says regarding the Jews who did not believe in the Christ: The Lord says: I will not show them my face, for I am the merciful Lord and I am existing until forever. Confess your sin and do not hide your fault, for you have sinned against the Lord, your God. Hear, O my brother, that he did not say: You have sinned against me, but against the Lord, your God, that is to say, against the Christ whom the Jews crucified. If they had believed in him, he would have made them live. Indeed, everyone who does not believe in him will not have eternal life.


[p. 54] {#po.13.p54}

84. The salvation operated by the Christ. - Furthermore, the prophet says regarding the Jews who believed in the Christ: The Lord will save Jacob and will deliver him from the hand of him who is stronger * than him. They will come to adore him on the mountain of Zion and they will rejoice in all his benefits, says the Lord. The prophet Jeremiah says regarding the children whom Herod killed, when the Christ was born: Thus says the Lord: A voice has been heard in Ramah, as well as weeping, lamentations, and groans. Rachel weeps for her children and does not want to cease the lamentations, for her children no longer exist for her. Thus says


[p. 55] {#po.13.p55}

the Lord: Your voice is weakened by weeping and your eyes by tears. But know that the reward of your works subsists. The reward is the Christ who increases their reward and remunerates them according to their works.
ናሁኬ ፡ ለበውከ፤ ፣ ኦዮስጥስ ፡ ከመ ፡ መጽእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወድኅነ ፡ እምስሕተተ ፡ ሰይጣን ፣ ወአምልኮ ፡ ጣዖት ፡ ወለአለሰ ፣ ቀተልዎሙ ፡ በቤተ ፣፡ ልሔም ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጥዩቀ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ”ክርስቶስ ፡ ይእስዮሙ ።፡፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ናሁ ፡ መዋፅል.፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እፌጽም ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ እስ ራኤል ፡ ወለቤተ ፡ ይሁዳ ። ውእተ ፡ አሚረ ፡ አሐንጽ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ መንበረ ፡ ጽድቅ ወይድኅን ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወኢየሩሳሌም ።፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኢይትኀጣእ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ቤተ ፡ እስራኤል =
Behold then that you have understood, O Justus, that the Christ has come and that we are delivered from the error of Satan and from the worship of idols. As for those whom they killed in Bethlehem in the days of King Herod, assuredly * the Christ has come to reward them. As the prophet says: Behold, days will come, says the Lord, when I will accomplish the Scriptures for the house of Israel and for the house of Judah. That day, I will build for David a throne of justice. Judah will be saved as well as Jerusalem. That is why there will not lack for the house of Judah the man who will sit on his throne, in the house of Israel.

85. The kingship of the Christ. - O my brother, if the Christ had not come, behold then that the prophets would have lied. As for us, we have stumbled and we are in error. Indeed, we have neither king, nor domination, nor kingdom; the word of the prophets has been accomplished upon us. If the Christ had not come, the kingdom of the Jews would remain as well as their domination as formerly. But since the Christ has come who was born of Mary, daughter of David, that is to say, of the Virgin, he is the * King


[p. 56] {#po.13.p56}
ቱ ፡ ን"ጉሥ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ እስራኤል ፡ ወዲበ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አሕዛብ =፡ ወይሰመይ ፡ ጻድቅ « እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ታለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፡ ወአለሰ ፡ ኦምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወተ ወክፍዎ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡፡፡
who will remain forever over Israel and over all the peoples; he is called the Just Lord; furthermore, he is the Word of the Lord. Those who have believed in him and have received him will remain in his kingdom for centuries of centuries.

86. The punishment reserved for the Jews who will not convert. - As the Lord says by the prophet: I will establish for them a New Law with the beasts of the desert and the birds of the heaven. The Jews who have believed in the Christ will become associated with the peoples in a single Testament. But those who have not believed, have not listened to the order of the Lord, and have set the table for the demons, those then will be scattered in all countries and will be destined for ruin and perdition until forever, as we see them today scattered and debased. Behold: understand, O my brother, that the Christ is son of David, that he is furthermore God and * that he is king over all the peoples and over the Jews who have believed in him. As the prophet Jeremiah says: The Lord has said: I will break the yoke of their neck and I will break their chains; they will no longer serve foreign gods, but they will serve the Lord, their God, and David, their king, whom I will raise up for them. But the Jews who have not believed in the Christ will become like troops of warriors who are weakened by the drunkenness of wine and who are of no use. They themselves will become associated,


[p. 57] {#po.13.p57}
እሉ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ይከውኑ ፣ ሱታፌ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገሃነም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሦሙ ፡ ሐሳዊ ፡ መ ሲሕ ፡ ዘተነበየ ፣ በእንቲ[አ]ሁ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይንእስ ፡ ቀርን ፡=
in Gehenna, with their king, the false Messiah regarding whom the prophet Daniel prophesied, by saying that he was the horn that is small.
ወመንግሥተ ፡ ዳዊትሰ ፡ ትበጽሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኢዮአቄም ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዮስያ ፡ ወተሐል ቅ =፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በአፈ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፡ “ነቢይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ከ መዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለኢዮአቂም ፡ ንጉሠ ፣ ይሁዳ ፡ ኢይትነሣእ ፡ እምዘርኡ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ምውት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተገድፈ ፡ ለላሕበ ፣መ ዐልት ፡ ወሞረ ፡ ሴሊት ፡ ወእሄውጽ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ አዝማዲሁ ፡ ወዲበ ፡ ደቁ = አስመ ፡ ነበርተ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፣ አመጽእ ፡ ዲቤሆሙ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ እኩ የ ፡ ዘነበብኩ ፡ እስመ ፡ አሙንቱሰ ፡ ኢይሰምኡ ፡ ቃልየ ፡=፡
The kingship of David will reach until Jehoiachin, son of Josiah, and will disappear. As the Lord says by the mouth * of the prophet Jeremiah regarding this: Thus has said the Lord to Jehoiachin, king of Judah: There will not rise from his posterity any man who will sit on the throne of David. His corpse has been thrown to the heat of the day and to the cold of the night. I will visit him, to punish him, him, his parents, and his children. Indeed, I will bring upon the inhabitants of Jerusalem, in the land of Judah, all the misfortunes of which I spoke to them, for they themselves do not listen to my word.

87. The New Covenant. - Have you heard, my brother Justus, what the prophet said regarding the kingship of David and of Judah? Behold now that it has become manifest that the Christ who was born of Mary, the Virgin, at Bethlehem of Judah, is therefore the son of David by the flesh, and that he is king over Judah and over the peoples who have believed in him. Behold then that the word * of the prophets has become certain, affirming that the kingship of the Jews has been abolished. The prophecy of our father Jacob has been accomplished: The domination will not disappear from Judah, nor the principality from his members, until he meets Him who is reserved for him and is the hope of the peoples. It is therefore the true Christ. He has

clothed himself with justice as a garment; he put the helmet of salvation upon his head; he put on the garment of vengeance; he wrapped himself in the cloak of punishment with which he will punish his enemies. Those who are in the west will fear the name of the Lord, and (those who are) in the rising sun (will fear) his glorious name. The punishment from the Lord will come like an impetuous torrent, in (his) anger. The Savior will come from Zion; he will take away the sin of Jacob. Behold (my) covenant (with) them, which (will be established) on my part, says the Lord: * My own Spirit will be upon you; I will put my word in your mouth; it will not depart from your mouth, (nor) from the mouth of your descendants, says the Lord, from now and forever. Furthermore he says: The Spirit of the Lord is upon me, he who has anointed me, so that I may gladden (with a happy message) the poor. He has sent me, so that I may heal the brokenhearted and preach liberty to the captives. Moreover he says: I will establish my covenant with them forever.

The prophet David says concerning the second coming of Christ: On that day, all the trees of the forests will rejoice before the face of the Lord, for he will come to judge the earth and judge the world in justice and the peoples in uprightness.

88. The misfortunes inflicted upon the Jews must incite them to convert. - Behold then that we must believe, so that * we may not be confounded, that we may not perish, and that the anger (of the Lord) may not come upon us. As the prophet Isaiah says: This people has intelligence, (but) it does not understand. The prophet Moses says: (This) people is foolish and not wise. The prophet Isaiah says concerning those who crucified Christ: Let all be confounded! Let those who rise up against (Christ) be covered with ignominy and let them go away confounded. The prophet Jeremiah says: Thus said the powerful Lord, God of Israel: Behold, I myself will give them torture by mouthfuls; I will give them gall to drink (as) water; I will scatter them in lands that neither they nor their fathers have known; I will send the sword upon them, until I have exterminated them by (the sword). Understand, O my brother Justus, that everywhere sadness has attached itself behind us * in this world and that we have been scattered in all lands, because we have not believed in Christ. We will descend into the eternal torment which will have no end. We must therefore weep and lament for ourselves, for we are banished and we are separated from the inheritance of all the saints and righteous; our portion will be with the sinners


[p. 60] {#po.13.p60}
