and the iniquitous. Furthermore, the groans and bitter tears will increase upon us, when we see Our Lord Jesus Christ coming, in great glory, with thousands of thousands of angels glorifying him with all the peoples. But we, we will be banished and removed from him. As Solomon says on this subject: Like foolish children they sent you (as) punishment for derision. * They who were not corrected by the amusement of derision will experience the punishment that is fitting from the Lord. Indeed, they themselves, because they will suffer, will be disgusted with those they called gods, while they are being punished. They will know that again they have been unfaithful to the knowledge of the Lord. Know (what) will take place. At the end of the punishment, (misfortune) will come upon them. Behold, for us, O my brother Justus, we have abandoned the way of uprightness and we have turned away from the justice that leads (men) to the port of salvation. Indeed, our eyes have darkened and have not been able to contemplate the light. The sun of justice is far from us; we have turned toward perversity and iniquity; that is why we have lost eternal life." 89. Conversion of Justus. - Justus said to Jacob: "You have spoken the truth, my brother, and everything you have expressed is written * in the Holy Scriptures." Then they returned to their houses.


[p. 61] {#po.13.p61}
አውሥኡ ፡ አኀው ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለዮስጥስ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ሰማዕነ ፡ ትካት ፡ ከመ ፡ ዐቢ ይ ፡ መምህር ፡ አንተ ፡ ወይእዜሰ ፡ እፎ ፡ ኢትነግረነ ፡ ዘሰማዕከ ፣ በኅበ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡፡ ተሠጥወ' ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ያአምር ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፡ አምላክ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩ ሎሙ ፣ ዘይቤ ፡ ጥዩቅ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወበመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነበበ ፡፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ አንሰ ፡ አብ ል ፡ ከመ ፡ ብብዝጎኀ ፡ ሣህሉ ፤ ወምሕረቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሠምረ ፡ ትጠመቁ ፡ እን ዘ፣0 ኢትፈቅዱ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አኮ ፡ ዘጸውዐክሙ ፡ አመ ፣ ኢተጠመቅሙ ፡ ለሊክ ሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ይኩን ፡ መድኀ“ኒ]ተክሙ ፣ ወመርሐክሙ ፡ ውስተ0 ብርሃን ፡፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡፡ ሕፅበኒ ፡ ወአንጽሐኒ ፡ እምኅጢአትየ ፡፡ ወእ ምአበሳየኒ ፣ አንጽሐኒ ።=፡ ወ"ኣካዕበ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ንዝሐኒ ፣ በአዛብ ፣ ወእነጽሕ = ተሐ ጽበኒ ፡ እም[በ]ገረድ ፤ እጸዐዱ ። ወኢሳይያስኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፣ ይ[ቤ ።= ተኀፀቡ ፣ ወንጹ ሓነ ፡ ኩኑ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በአፈ ፡ ሕዝቅያል ፡ ነቢይ ።፡ እነዝኅኀክሙ ፡ በማይ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ወትነጽሑ ፣ እምሐጢአትክሙ ፡ ወአንሰ ፡ ኦአኀው ፡ ናሁ ፡ አመንኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውኣቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘበአማን ፣ ዘመጽአ ፣፡ ወተወልደ ፡ አማርያም ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ በቤተ ፡ ልሔም ፡ ዘይሁዳ ፡፡ ናሁ ፡ አምይ[አ]ዜሰ ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ አእኩ ትዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፤ አምላክክሙ ፡፡
The brothers answered and said to Justus: "As for us, we learned long ago that you are a great teacher. But now why do you not explain to us what you have learned from Jacob?" He answered and said to them: "The Lord, the living God, knows that all (the things) that (Jacob) said are certain and that he spoke by the Holy Spirit. But I, I say that in the greatness of his clemency and his mercy the Lord has deigned that you be baptized, even though you did not want (it) yourselves. If it were not he who had called you, you would not have been baptized of your own accord. Indeed, he himself wanted to become your salvation and led you into the light. As the prophet David says: Wash me; purify me from my sins and cleanse me from my faults. * Furthermore he says: Sprinkle me with hyssop and I will become pure. You will wash me and I will become whiter than snow. The prophet Isaiah says: Wash yourselves and become pure. The Lord says through the mouth of the prophet Ezekiel: I will sprinkle you with pure water and you will be purified from your sins. As for me, O brothers, behold I have believed that He who came and was born of Mary, the Holy Virgin, in Bethlehem of Judah, is the true Christ. Behold, from now on the truth has become manifest to you; give thanks to the Lord, your God.


[p. 62] {#po.13.p62}
ናሁ ፡ እነግረክሙ ፡ አነ ፡ ዕፁበ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ሀሎኩ ፡ በዐካ ፡ ሀገር ፣ አ መ ፡ ይወፅኡ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ፋርስ ፣ ይቅትልዎሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ተን ሥኡ ፡ አይሁድ ፤ ወአውዐዩ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወቀተሉ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ሕዝበ ። ወቦእ ምውስቴቶሙ ፡ ፳ ፡ ”ዲያቆን ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሌዎንቴዎስ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ በሕገ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወናሁ ፣ አኀዝዎ ፣ አይሁድ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፡ አሕመምዎ ፣ ወአመንደብዎ ፡ወ ቀሠፍዎ = ወእምብዝጎኅ ፡ ጻማ ፡ ወኩነኔ ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ፡ ክሕደ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አይሁዳዊ =፡ ወእ ምድኅረ ፣ ኾንዱይ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ረከቦ ፡ ፳ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ኖኅተ ፡ ምዙራቦ ሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ትነብር ፡ ዝየ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሐነቆ ፡ አንብእ ፡ ወብካ ይ ፡፡ ይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ረሳእከኑ ፣ ዘይቤ ፡ አግዚአነ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ዘክህደኒ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እክህዶ ፣ አነሂ ፣ በቅድመ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ዘበ ፣ ሰማያት ።= ወናሁ ፡ አንተሰ ፣፡ ክሕድ ከ ፡ ወኮንከ ፡ አይሁዳዊ ፡ ሊተሰ ፡ ይመስለኒ ፡ ዘትቀንእ ፡ አንተ ፡ በሕገ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘከማከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡፡ ወሰሚያዖ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ደንገጸ ፡ ወኅሠሠ ፣ ያምስጥ ፡ ”እምእዴሆሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ ወስእነ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ዐርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልፁል፣ጥ ቅመ ፡ ቅጽረ ፡ ሀገር ፣ ወተወረወ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወሞተ ወርእዮሙ ፡ አ
90. Justus confirms through the story of the deacon Leontius that Christ is the King of Israel. - Behold, I (am going to) tell you myself a surprising fact that happened, while I was in the city of 'Akà [Acca], when the Persians left, to kill the people of the Christians. Then the Jews rose up, burned the church, and killed many people. There was among (the Christians) a * deacon, called Lëwontêwos [Leontius], a wise ascetic of the law of Christ. Behold, the Jews arrested him, in order to kill him: they made him suffer, mistreated him, and scourged him. Because of the greatness of the torments and the tortures that (were inflicted) upon him, he apostatized and became a Jew. Many days later, he met an old man sitting at the gate of the synagogue of the Jews. He said to him: "Why are you sitting here?" Then tears and weeping choked him. The old man said to him: "Have you forgotten what Our Lord said in the Gospel: Whoever will have denied me before men, I also will deny him before my Father who is in heaven? Now behold, you yourself have apostatized and become a Jew. But it seemed to me that you were zealous yourself in the law of Christ, for no one was like you among the Christians." Having heard this, (Leontius) was terrified and sought to flee * from the hand of the Jews; (but) he could not. Then he climbed onto the high wall of the city ramparts, threw himself into the sea, and died. Having seen this, the Jews were saddened


[p. 63] {#po.13.p63}
ይሁድ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሐዘኑ ፤ ፈድፋደ ፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮኖሙ ፡ ትምክህተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘክሀደ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከማሆሙ ፣ አይሁዳዊ ፡፡
extremely, for he was for them (a subject) of pride, because he had apostatized and had become, like them, Jews.
ወአምዝ ፡ ርእየ ፡ በሕልም ፡ ፳ ፡ ብአሷ ፡ አምካህናቶሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ ሰቡዐ፣ለያል የ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንተ ፡ አውአይዋ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትሐነጽ ፡ በገወርቅ፤ ወበባሕርይ ፡ ዕንቀ- ፡ ክቡር ፣ ወትበርሕ ፣ ከመ ፣፤ ፀሐይ ፡ ወምኩራቦሙሰ ፣ ለአይሁ ድ ፡ ተሰጥመት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፣ ወወረደት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽዩእ ፣ ኩስሕ ።፡ ወሀሎ ፣ በ ዐካ ፡ ሀገር ፣ ብእሲ ፡ አይሁዳዊ ፡ ፈራሄ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፣ ወይትባረ ኩ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አይሁድ ፡=፡ ወሰሚዖ ፡ ሕልመ ፡ ዘሐስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ካህን ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሕልም ፣ ዘይከውን ፣ ንስተቶሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘየአምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ክ ርስቲያን ፡ ንጉሦሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡፤
91. Vision of Isaac. - Then a man from among the priests of the Jews saw in a dream, for seven nights, the church that the Jews had burned. Behold, it was (re)built (of) gold, of gems (and of) precious stones, and it shone like the sun; but the synagogue of the Jews was overturned to the ground and had fallen upon stinking excrement. There was in the city of Acca a Jew fearing the Lord, called Isaac; * the Jews asked him for his blessing. Having learned of the dream that the priest had had, he said: "This dream (signifies) the destruction of the Jews that will occur, for the Christ in whom the Christians believe is the king of Israel."
ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ ዕደው ፡ ወተቃረንዎ ፣ ለይስሐቅ ፣ ወተባሐሉ ፡ በበይናቲሆ ሙ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ እምከመ ፡ ተሰምዐ ፡፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፣ የዐልዉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ አይሁድ፣ ወይከውኑ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ። ወእምዝ ፣ ተማከሩ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፣ ወመስዮ ፡ ቦኡ ፣ ውስተ፣ ቤቱ ፡ ወረከብቻዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፣ ባሕቲቱ ፣ አሐዝዎ ፣ ወቀተልዎ ፡ ወኢተሰምዐ ፡ ዜ ናሁ ፣ አላ ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ ሐሰዉ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ግብተ ፡ ሞተ ፡= ወእምዝ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡ዮስ ጥስ ፡ ያአምር ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ "” ኦአኀው ፡ ከመ ፡ ነገረኒ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘቀ
Then five men rose up, struggled against Isaac, argued among themselves, and said: "As soon as this word is heard, all the Jews will apostatize and become Christians." Then they decided among themselves to kill him. Evening having come, they entered his house and found him sitting alone; they seized him and killed him. The story (of this Jew) was not learned; but only they lied and said: "He died suddenly." Then Justus said to them: "The Lord knows, * O brothers, that the man who was killed spoke to me


[p. 64] {#po.13.p64}
ተሎ ፡ አንትሙሰ ፡ ኢትሕዝኑ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዕልው ፡ አስመ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ይቤ፣ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘመጽአ ፣ ንጉሠ ፣ እስራኤል ፣ ውእቱ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡፣ዘንተ ፡ አአ ኩትዎ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወአተዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡=፡
on this subject. As for you, do not be saddened about this apostate, for he himself said that the Christ who has come is the king of Israel." Having heard this, they gave thanks and they glorified the Lord. (Then) they returned to their houses.

### EIGHTH ASSEMBLY
ሳምን ፡ ጉባኤ ፣ ወካዕበ ፣፡ ተጋብኡ ፣ ይቤሎ ፡ ዮስጥስ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፣፡ ኦእጉየ ፣ እመሰ ፣ ደክሙ ፣ ሰብአ ፣፡ ሮም ፣፡ ወተመውፁ ፡ ይትነሥኡ ፡፣ ፲አቅርንት ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወእምድኅሬሁ ፡ ሐሳዊ ፣ መሲሕ ፣ ወባሕቱ ፣ አሌሎሙ ፡፣ ለእለ ፡ ይትወከፍዎ ።፡ በ ከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፣ ነቢይ ፡= ከመዝ ፣ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ርጉመ ፡ ለይኩን ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ ዘይትአመን ፣ በእጓ"ለ ፣ አገመሕያው ፡ ወያሰምክ ፡ መዝራዕቶ ፡ ቦ ቱ ፡፡ እስመ ፡ እምእግዚአብሔር ፡ ርሕቀ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ ከመ ፡ ኳሂላ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዘኢይሬኢ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዕበ ፣ ይመጽእ ፡ ወይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ በድው ፡ ዘአል ቦ ፡ ዘይነብሮ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ውእተ ፣ አሚረ ፣ ይቤልዎሙ ፡ ለዝ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወለ
92. On the Antichrist. - Again they gathered. Justus said to Jacob: "O my brother, if the Romans have been weakened and have been defeated, the ten horns of which the prophet Daniel spoke will arise. Then (will come) the false Messiah. But woe to those who will receive him! As the prophet Jeremiah says: Thus said the Lord: Cursed be every man who trusts in * man and leans his arm upon him, for his heart is far from the Lord! He will become like a heath in the desert, (he) who will not see happiness when it comes, and he will dwell in the desert where no one dwells. Furthermore he says: On that day, it will be said to this people and to


[p. 65] {#po.13.p65}
ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ጋኔን ፣ መስሕት ፣ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወለተ ፡ ሕዝብየ ፡ አኮ ፡ ለ ንጽሕ ፤ ወአኮ ፡ ለቅዳሴ ፣ ዳአሙ ፡ ለጋኔን =፡ ወይእዜሰ ፡ አነ ፣ እነግሮሙ ፣ ቀሥት የ ፡ናሁ ፡ የዐርግ! ፡ ከመ ፡ ደመና ፡ ወከመ ፡ ዐውሎ ፡ ሰረገላሁ ፡ ይበድር ፡ እምነ ፡ አን ስርት ፡ አፍራሷሁ ።፡ አልብነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኅሰርነ ፡ ኅፀቢ ፡ እከየ ፣ ልብኪ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ ፣ ትድኀኒ ፡ እስከ ፣ ማእዜኑ ፣ ትሄሊ ፣ ላዕሌኪ ፣ ሕሊና ፣ ዘያሰርኀኪ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘይዜንወኪ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዳን ፡ ወያሰምእ" ፡ ሕማመ ፡ አምአድባረ ፡ ኤፍሬም ፡=፡
Jerusalem: A seductive demon is on the way of the desert; the daughter of my people (gives herself) not to purity and not to sanctification, but to the demon. But now I myself will speak to them: Behold, my bow will rise like the clouds; the chariots (of the seducer) will be like a whirlwind; his horses will be faster than eagles. We have no more (strength), for we have been overwhelmed (with trials). Wash the malice of your heart, Jerusalem, so that you may be saved. How long will you meditate * within yourself thoughts that torment you? A voice has come from Dan that announces (misfortune) to you and (that) makes affliction heard from the mountains of Ephraim.
እስመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለሐሳዊ ፡ መሲሕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ምጽ[አገቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢ የሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ዘመጽአ ፣ ከመ ፡፣ ጠል ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ጸምር ፡ ናሁ ፣ ኤርሞላኦስ ፣ ዘ ውእቱ ፣ ሐሳዊ ፡ መሲሕ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ፍርሃት ፣፡ ወረአድ ፣ ወዕፁብ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወይገብር ፡ ሀከከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወበቀዳሜ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ሠናየ ፣ በተኬንዎ ፡፡ ወኩሉ ፣ ግብሩ ፡ ጐሕሉት ፣ ወዓመፃ ፡፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ፣ ዳንኤል ፡ ነቢይ ።፡ ወይነብብ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ልዑል ፡ ወያስሕቶሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳነ፣ ልዑል ፡ ወይሔሊ ፡ ይዌልጥ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወሕገ ፡ ወይትወሀብ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕድሜሁ ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ወ፪ዓመት ፣ ወመንፈቀ ፡ ዓመት = ወነበረ ፣ ዐውዶ ፡ ወሰዐሮ ፡ መልክክ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያማስኖ ፡ ለግሙራ ።፡ ወክዕበ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በእንተ ፡
Indeed, the coming of the false Messiah is not like the coming of Our Lord Jesus Christ who came like dew upon the wool. Behold, 'Ermolä'os, that is to say the false Messiah, will come in great fear, (in) trembling, and (in) a violent torment; he will cause trouble on the earth; at the beginning of his coming, he will appear to men as if he were doing good, (this) by artifice; (but) all his work is cunning and iniquity. As the prophet Daniel says: He will utter words against the Most High; he will seduce the saints of the Most High; he will propose to change the days and the Law; (the saints) will be given to him * until the time (fixed) for him; they will be in his hand (for) one year, two years, and half a year. An angel will dwell around him and will annihilate him, in order to destroy him forever. Furthermore, the prophet Jeremiah says concerning the malice of his thoughts: It is


[p. 66] {#po.13.p66}
እከየ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፣፤ አነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘአሬኢ ፡ ልበ ፡ ወእፈትን ፣ ዙልያተ ፡ ከመ ፡አ ሀቦ ፡ ለስ፳ ፡ በከመ ፣ ፍኖቱ ፡ ወበከመ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ምግበሩ ። ነቀወት ፣፡ ቆቃሕ ፡ ወአስተጋ ብአት ፡ ዘኢወለደት ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ብዕሎ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወየሐድጎ' ፡ በመንፈቀ ፣ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ወይከውን ፡፣ አብደ ፣ በደኃሪቱ ።፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፤ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ውእ ተ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ያመጽእ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መጥባሕተ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወጽኑዐ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ከይሲ ፣ ወአርዌ ፣ ምድር ፡ የዐኪ ፡ ወይቀትሎ ፡ ለከይሲ ፣ ”ዘውእቱ ፡ በገባሕር ፡፡ ተሰጥወ ፡ ዮስጥስ ፣ ወይቤ ፣ እሙነ ፡ ትቤ ፣ ወባሕቱ ፣ አሌሎሙ ፡ ለአለ ፣ ይትወ
I the Lord who see the heart and probe the reins, in order to give to each according to his way and according to the fruit of his works. (As) the partridge clucks and gathers what it has not begotten, (so does) he who amasses his riches outside of justice; he will leave them in the midst of his days and he will become foolish at the end (of his life). As the prophet Isaiah says: On that day, the Lord will bring a great and strong sword against the dragon and the serpent (which) is wicked, and he will kill the dragon * which is (in) the sea. Justus answered and said: "You speak the truth. But woe to those who will receive the wicked dragon, that is to say 'Ermolä'os, the false Messiah!"

93. The Church and the hierarchy. - Jacob said: "O my brother Justus, understand that the Christ who was born of Mary, of the Holy Virgin, in Bethlehem of Judah, is therefore the Christ concerning whom the Law and the prophets announced, (the Christ) who came after the sixty-nine weeks. As the Lord says to the prophet Daniel: (Seventy weeks have been fixed), so that sin might be consumed, that crime might be sealed, that fault might be erased, that iniquity might be forgiven, that eternal justice might come, that the vision of the prophets might be sealed, and that the Holy of Holies might be anointed. * You will know and you will understand. Yes certainly, my brother, the churches of Christ are extremely numerous on earth, from the rising sun to the setting and from the north to the south, in which are kept the Law and the Testament (and) toward which he has gathered all the peoples, so that they may glorify and thank the Lord. Behold, the word of the prophets who prophesied has been fulfilled. Furthermore, in (the churches) there are the clerics, the primates, the eminent teachers, the priests, the deacons, and the apostles who order the people to accomplish justice and uprightness and teach them * the Law and the Testament of the Lord. As the prophet Ezekiel says: I will set over them one (shepherd who) will feed them, my servant David; he will become for them their shepherd. I myself the Lord, I will become for them their God, and David in their midst will be their prince. I myself the Lord, I have spoken; I will establish for David a Testament of peace and I will make the wicked animals disappear from the earth. The prophet David says: Your priests will be clothed with justice and your righteous will rejoice with happiness. Furthermore he says: There I will make a horn sprout for David; I will prepare

a lamp for my Messiah; I will clothe his enemies with ignominy; in him my holiness will flourish. O my brother Justus, behold the prophets announced long ago concerning the priests and the New Testament. As the Scripture says: I will make them powerful, * so that they may judge with justice over all the peoples and the princes of the earth. Behold then, my brother, that the Gospel of Our Lord has been heard in the whole world by the preaching of his holy disciples. The prophet David says concerning the Church: Listen, my daughter; see and lend your ear; forget your people and the house of your father, for the king has desired your beauty; indeed, he is your Lord. The daughters of Tyre will prostrate themselves before him with gifts. Furthermore he says concerning the holy disciples: In place of your fathers children are born to you; you will call them princes of all the earth.

listened to me and they have not lent their ear, so as not to turn away from evil and so as not to (cease) to offer incense to foreign gods. In the morning, my anger and my punishment were inflamed at the gate of Judah and outside of Jerusalem (which) has become a desert and a field until today. All this, we have seen and we have known. Justus answered and said: "Behold, everything that was said has been fulfilled upon the Jews, for they did not believe in Christ and did not * adhere to him. As the prophet Isaiah says: Say to this people: Listen well, (but) you will not understand; see well, (but) you will not know. Indeed, the heart of this people has become thick; they have turned away their ears, so as not to hear; they have closed (their eyes), so as not to see with their eyes; (they have stopped their ears), so as not to hear with their ears; (they have thickened their heart), so as not to understand with their heart. They will not convert and I will not be merciful toward them. The prophet Jeremiah says: It will be said to this people and to Jerusalem: A seductive demon is on the way of the desert; the daughter of my people (gives herself) not to purity and not to sanctification, but to the seductive demon. But now I myself will say to them: Behold, my bow will rise like the clouds; the chariots (of the seducer) will be like a whirlwind; (his horses) will be faster than eagles. * Jacob said to Justus: "This

prophecy is concerning 'Ermolä'os, the false Messiah whom the prophet Daniel called the horn that is small. Indeed, the Jews who did not believe in the true Christ will receive the wicked dragon and will believe in him. As the prophet says: The Spirit of justice will come and they will not receive him; (but) when the spirit of the demon has come, they will receive him and they will treat him as God. Behold, they are covered with ignominy and they are ruined, because they did not receive Our Lord Jesus Christ, but on the contrary (because) they followed the wicked dragon, impure and seductive. As the prophet Isaiah says: They have set the table for the demons." Justus said: "You speak the truth, (in affirming) that this word was said concerning the Jews who do not believe in Christ. But from now on we must no longer live * in the Law of the Jews." Then they continued (the discussion) in their houses, after they had recognized and were certain that the Lord was leading them into (the law of) love.

95. Vision of Justus. - Joseph went away with Justus into his house and said to him: "Will you not explain to me what Jacob said?" Justus said: "The Lord is living! (I affirm) that the Holy Spirit has dwelt upon Jacob. That is why he has repudiated the love of food and drink and has devoted himself to the

ጸሎተ ወዕቤሃ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ዮስጥስ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እነግረከ ፡ ኦዮሴፍ ፡ ከመ፣፡ሊተ፣ ዮም ፡ ዐሥር ፡ ወኅሙስ ፡ ለያልይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እሬኢ ፣ በሕልም ፡ ከመ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወዙሎ ሙ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወያዕቆብኒ ፡ ኅቡር ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፣ ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ያስተበፅዕዎ ። ወባሕ ቱ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይፈቆቅድ ፡ አስምዖ ፡ ዘን”ተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትመካሕ ፡ ወኢያዕቢ ፡ ርእዕ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዳእሙ ፣ ዘእሴአሎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስ ቶስ ።=
fasting and to prayer." Immediately Justus became sad and wept. He said: "I (am going to) tell you, O Joseph, that (behold) for me today (it has been) fifteen nights (during which) I am seeing in a dream Moses and all the prophets; Jacob also is associated with them; as for them, they proclaim him blessed. But for me, I do not want to tell him this * thing, so that he may not glorify himself and that he may not exalt himself; but only (I want him to explain to me) that upon which I will question him concerning the coming of Our Lord Jesus Christ."

### NINTH ASSEMBLY

96. On the end of the world and the second coming of Christ. - A few days later, they gathered again. Justus said to Jacob: "Behold, the prophets recount that a great catastrophe will occur in the last days." Jacob answered and said: "Yes, my brother, a plague will occur, such as there has not been the like. Indeed, the prophet Isaiah says: Behold, the day of the Lord will come which is full of punishment and anger, in order to annihilate the world and to destroy the sinners. The stars of heaven will no longer shine for anyone; the sun, in rising, will darken; the moon will no longer shine. I will no longer govern * the whole world." Furthermore he says: "The heaven will be rolled up like a

book; all the stars of heaven will fall, as (fall) the leaves of the vine and as fall the leaves of the fig tree. Furthermore he says: Indeed, it is the day of the judgment of the Lord and it is the year of the rendering of the judgment of Zion. Its valleys will convert and will change themselves (into pitch); its earth will become sulfur; its earth will burn day and night like pitch and will never be extinguished; the smoke will rise on high; (Zion) will be ruined for generations of generations and will be destroyed for long days. Indeed, thus says the prophet concerning those who do not believe in Christ. After the ten horns have disappeared, 'Ermolä'os, the false Messiah, will come. The people of the Jews will avenge him. As the prophet says: They will go out, to see the seducer (in the midst of his corpses) whose worms * will not sleep and whose fire will not be extinguished. This will be an example for every being (endowed) with flesh.

97. The day of judgment will be dreadful. - That day, a great punishment will occur. As (says) the prophet Joel: Woe to me on that day, for the day of the Lord approaches. The prophet Zephaniah says: It approaches and it hastens greatly, the noise of the day of the Lord. It is bitter, evil, and hard. It is a day of calamity, a day of affliction and torment, a day of terror and perdition, a day of darkness and gloom, a day of clouds and fog, a day of carnage and cries (of war). Furthermore Solomon says: Remove

the anger from your heart and cast away the evil from your flesh. Furthermore he says: (Remember the Lord), before the days of misfortune come and the years arrive in which you will say: "(This) did not happen to me by my fault", before the sun, * the light, the moon, and the stars darken, and before the clouds return after the rain. The prophet Micah says: I will exercise (my) vengeance (on) the peoples in my anger and in my punishment, for they do not listen to me certainly. (Behold) the word of the Lord that the Lord has said: The Lord has risen (and) has pleaded against his people. The prophet Isaiah says: Is it not the Lord and his (punishment)? They refused to go in his way and they did not listen to his Law. He brought upon them the punishment of his anger and he thundered upon them. Listen, for he said: (not) the punishment of my anger, but on the contrary the punishment of the anger of Christ, Son of the living Lord.
ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፣ ናሁ ፡፣ ነጸራ ፤ ለምድር ፡ ወኢሀለወት ፡ ወለሰማይኒ ፡ ወኢከ መ፣ ምን”ት፣ ወኢያስተርኢ ፡ ብርሃኑ ፡ ወርእዮሙ ፡ ለአድባር ፡ ወደንገፁ ፡ ወሕኑጻት ፡ አህጉር ፣ መዝበር ፣ እ[ም]ቅድመ ፣፡ ገጹ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ትን
98. Christ will judge men. - Furthermore the prophet says: Behold, he looked at the earth and it no longer existed; the heaven, and (it became) like nothingness; * its light will no longer appear; he saw the mountains and they were terrified; the cities built (became) ruins before the face of the Lord. The prophet David says a prophecy concerning Christ: O Lord, the king

Furthermore he says: May your hand find all your adversaries! May your hand go before all your enemies and may it render them like a furnace of fire for the time (when you will show) your face, O Lord! In your anger shake them. May the fire devour them! Destroy their fruit from the earth and their posterity from among the sons * of man. Indeed, they have schemed iniquity against you and they have conceived a design that they cannot realize.

99. How Christ will come. - You have heard, O brothers, the punishment and the torment that will occur for those who do not believe in Christ. Behold then that he will come in great glory. As the prophet Isaiah says: He will kill the dragon that is in the sea. Furthermore he says: Behold, the name of the Lord will come in a distant day; (his) anger will be inflamed with the glory of the word of his lips and with the punishment of his word, and the punishment of his anger will devour like fire. (It will be exercised) against 'Ermolä'os, that is to say the false Messiah, when the Lord comes with the

ሰማያት ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፣ እምኔነ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይክል ፣ ርእዮቶ ፣ ለወልደ ፡እ ግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ በሥጋ ። እስመ ፣ ይመጽእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ”መብረቅ ፡ ዘይ ሠርቅ ፣ እምጽባሕ ፡ እስከነ ፡ ዐረብ ፡=፡

As the prophet Daniel says: Behold, there came on the clouds of heaven one like the Son of man, and he arrived before the Ancient of days. It is manifest that he will come in the flesh which he took from us. Furthermore (Daniel) says: To him were given dominion, glory, and kingship; all peoples, countries, and tribes will serve him; his dominion will be an eternal dominion that will not pass away, and his kingdom will be (a kingdom) that will not be destroyed. The prophet David says: The Lord will come manifestly; our God will not keep silent; fire will burn before him. Moreover he says: Flame will surround his enemies. The prophet Moses says: Indeed, * the Lord will judge his people. Behold then: see, brothers, the torment that the Lord has prepared for those who do not believe in Christ, (and know) that it will have no end."
ይቤ ፣ ዮስጥስ ፣ እሙነ ፡ ትቤ ፣ እጉየ ፡ እምይ[አ]ዜሰ ፡ አልቦ ፡ እንከ ፡ ዘንፀንሕ ፣ እምድኅረ ፣ ጸንዐት ፣ መንግሥተ ፣ ሮም ፡፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ ራብዕ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ናሁ ፣ ይከውን ፤
100. The little horn foretold by Daniel. - Justus says: "You speak the truth, my brother. Henceforth we will no longer wait for another (Christ). After the kingdom of the Romans has been powerful, that is to say the fourth beast, behold


[p. 76] {#po.13.p76}
ሀከከ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ዙሉ ፣ አሕዛብ ፡ ወይመጽኡ ፡ ፲አቅርንት ፡=፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወዝኩሂ ፡ ካልዕ ፡ ቀርን ፤ ዘበፄለ ፡ ወመልጥፕሙ ፡ ለእልክቱ ፡ ርአእለ ፡ እምቅድ]ሜሁ ፡ አቅርንት ፣ ዝኩ ፡ ዘቦ ፣ አዕይንተ ፣ ወአፉሁ ፣ ይነብብ ፡ ዓቢያተ ፡ ወራእይሂ ፡የዓቢ ፡ እ ምቢጹ ፡ ርኢክዎ ፣ ለውእቱ ፣ ቀርን ፣ ዘይንእስ ፡ ወይትቃተሎሙ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ”ወአለ ሰ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይንእስ ፣ ቀርን ፡ ወይትወክፍዎ! ፣ ድልው ፣ ሎሙ ፡ ዙነኔ ፡ ወየሐ ውሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡=፡
that trouble will arise among all peoples and the ten horns will come. (Behold) what the prophet Daniel says: (There arose) another horn which grew and tore out the three horns that were before it. This (horn) had eyes; its mouth uttered arrogant (words); its appearance was greater than (that) of its fellows. I saw this horn which is little: it was making war on the saints. * As for those who will believe in the horn which is little and will receive it, the torment will be ready for them and they will go with it into the eternal fire."

101. No other Christ will come. - Again Jacob answered and said to him: "Do you not remember what we heard long ago in Tiberias regarding a priest from among the priests of the Jews? (Do you not recall) how he said: 'In eight years the Christ, king of Israel, will come; he will be born of a Virgin. It is he, therefore, who will raise up our kingdom and strengthen our people.' Those who heard said thus: 'Blessed will be the Virgin * from whom the Christ will be born, and blessed will be his father and his mother.'"
ተሠጥወ ፡ ዮስጥስ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ እወ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምኡ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ይቤሉ ፣ ሐሰወ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ካህን ፡= ወካዕበ ፣ ይቤሉ ፡ ናሁ ፣ መጽአ ፤

102. The prophecy of Daniel is fulfilled. - Jacob said to him: "You say (a thing that is) certain. Yes, it has been so. Behold then that we have recognized and understood what the prophet Daniel says: A stone was cut from the mountain without (the help of) a hand, and the image of gold, silver, iron, bronze, and terracotta was broken. Behold, the Lord has revealed to us through his prophets everything that will happen in the last days."

103. Prediction of the resurrection of the dead. - Justus says: * "It is written thus in the book of the prophet Daniel. Moreover he says: That day, all (those of) your people who are found (written) in the book will be saved. Many who sleep in the dust of the earth will rise for eternal life, but (others) for eternal reproach and shame. Those who have been wise will shine like the light of the sun, and many among the righteous (will shine) like the stars forever." Jacob said to him: "Know that the second coming of Christ and the resurrection of the dead will happen, according to what the prophet says: I will raise the dead who are lying in the earth and those who have grown old in the dust. 104. Prediction of the second coming of Christ. - When the Christ, Son of the living Lord, comes for the second (time) in great glory, (this) will happen like lightning. As the prophet Habakkuk says: His brightness is like the light; horns are in his hands. The coming of Christ will be neither hidden nor secret, when the armies of the angels appear with him in fear, * in trembling and dread. As the prophet Daniel says: Power has been given to him; as for his kingdom, it will be a kingdom for ages of ages. Moreover the prophet Isaiah says: Behold, the Lord will come like the flame of fire; furthermore his hand will be raised; it will burn sinners like fire and like dry straw that the fire devours; as the straw of the fields burns and (as) it devours the hills that are around it, so the punishment of the Lord will consume the whole earth. The prophet Malachi says: Behold that (the Christ) will come, has said the

The prophet David says: Fire will go * before him and flame will surround his enemies. His lightnings will appear to the world. Indeed, he himself is the light and he himself created the lights and the lightnings. As the prophet David says: (It is) he who looks at the earth and makes it tremble, he who touches the mountains and they smoke. Behold, the Christ, the true Lord, has come. (As he came) himself the first (time), so he will come the second (time) in the last days, from the east, in great glory. His cross, his wounds, the traces of the nails of his hands and feet, and the perforation of his side will appear. As the prophet Zechariah says: They shall look on him whom they have pierced."
አሜሃ ፡ ይኔጽሩ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ እለ፣ ኢየአምኑ "። በክርስቶስ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይመጽእ ፡ በዓ ቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ፡=፡ ውእተ" ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ይበክዩ ፡ ወይላሕዉ ፡ ወይብሉ ፣ ”ዝውእቱ ፡ ዘሞ ቃሕናሁ" ፣ ወኩራዕናሁ' ፣ ወቀናእነ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወሐመይናሁ ፡፣ ወነበብነ" ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሐሰተ ፡ ሰቀልናሁ ፤ ወቀተልናሁ ፡ ወዓዲ ፣ ይብሉ ፣ ደቂቆሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ ዝውእ
105. Necessity of faith in Christ. - Then the Jews who do not believe in Christ will look at (him) coming in great glory. At that moment, they will weep, lament, and say: * "It is he whom we have bound, whom we have slapped, against whom we have burned (with anger), whom we have insulted, against whom we have uttered lies, whom we have crucified and whom we have killed." of the Jews will say: "It is he whom our foolish fathers crucified; as for us, we did not believe in him, but on the contrary we denied him, we outraged him, and we mocked him. Behold then that we have sinned and that we have wandered, for we did not recognize Our Lord Jesus Christ." But the righteous will stand before him, in joy and in gladness. Furthermore Jacob says to the brothers: "We must not despise the Christ who came for the salvation of the whole world, so that we do not fall into great error, but on the contrary (we must) believe in Christ, have confidence in him, and lean upon him, since he is the Son of the * Lord, the King of glory, the Lord and the God of those who believe in his own name and are baptized in the Holy Spirit." Having heard this word, the brothers cried out and said: "We glorify the Lord who has revealed to us the mysteries of holiness and has granted us to know his Messiah. Therefore let us thank him, bless him, and sanctify him, for he himself does not want the death of the sinner, but (his) conversion and (his) return." 106. New testimonies on the second coming of Christ. - Justus says to Jacob: "Behold, I myself have made you swear by the living Lord to reveal to me the second coming of Christ." Jacob answered and said to him: "You yourself surpass me in knowledge, for you are a doctor and you know

The prophet David says: The face of the Lord is against those who do evil, in order to extirpate their memory from the earth."

Justus says to Jacob: "You speak well. But explain this word to me." Jacob answered and said to him: "The prophet Isaiah says: I am living, says the Lord! Indeed, every knee will bow before me and every tongue will supplicate the Lord. The prophet Jeremiah says: I am the Lord, (I) who see the heart and probe the kidneys, in order to give to each according to his way and according to the fruit of his works. Moreover the prophet says: I am living, says the Lord, * Creator of all and Author of all! (I will render to each) according to his works."

107. Disbelief in Christ will be punished. - Behold: henceforth, my brother, do not be of little faith in Christ, Son of the Lord, for he himself must judge the family of men. As the prophet David says: Indeed, clemency is (the property) of the Lord, for you will render to each according to his works. Understand then, my brother, how this prophecy has manifested

ሴ ፡ ለአብ ፣ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ! ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ርትእ ። ወይ ፈድዮ ፡ ለኩሉ ፣ በከመ ፣ ምግባሩ ፡=፡ ወበእንተዝ ፣ ንትአቀብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢንትጐለቅ ፣ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢየ[ኦ]ምኑ" ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢንኩን ፡ ለኅጐል ፡ ወለሙስና ።፡ ወ እመሰ ፤ አለውነ ፤ ወክህድናሁ ፣ ንርሕቅ ፣ እምእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢይሰርቅ ፡ለነ ፡ ፀሓ የ ፤ ጽድቅ በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፣ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡፡ ”ናሁ ፣ አንተሂ ፡ ተመዓዕከነ" ፣ ወን ሕነሂ ፡ አበስነ ፣ በበይነ ፣ ዝንቱ ፣ ስሕትነ ፡ ወኮነ ፣ ርኩሳነ ፣ ዮልነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀርቀ ፡ ትክ ቶ ፣ ወኩሉ ፡ ጽድቅነ ። ወተገደፍነ ፣ ከመ ፣ ቁጽል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀጢአትነ ፡ ከመ፣፡ነ ፋስ ፣ ሐፈሰነ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘጸውዐ ፡ ስመከ ፡ ወኢዘከሩከክ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሜጥከ ፡ ገጸከ ፡ እም ኔነ ፣ ወመጠውከነ ፡ ለኀጢአትነ" ፡፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወንሕነ ፡ ፅ ቡር ፡ ወአንተ ፣ ፈጠርከነ ፡ ወተግባረ ፡ እደዊከ ፣፡ ዙልነ ፡ ወኢትትመዐዐነ ፡ ወኢትዝክ ር ፡ ለነ ፡ ኅጢአተነ ።፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ ነጽረነ" ፡ እስመ ፣ ሕዝብከ ፡ ንሕነ ። ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ንጉሠ ፣ ስብሐት ፣ ረከቡኒ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢኅሠሠኒ ፤ ወጸውውዑኒ ፡ እለ ፣ ኢያአም ሩኒ ፡=፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ኢላይያስ ፣ ነቢይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢየአምኑ" ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡=፡ ጸዋዕክዎሙ ፣ ወተጸመሙኒ ፡ ወተናገርክዎሙ ፣ ወኢያውሥኡኒ ፣ ወገብሩ ፣ አኩየ፣ በቅድሜየ ።፡ ወእምዝ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አንትሙሰ ፡ መክርክሙ ፡ እኩ
the Holy Trinity of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit. Indeed, (the Christ) is the Judge of righteousness: he will render to each according to his works. That is why let us take care not to be numbered with those who do not believe in Christ, so as not to be (destined) for perdition and ruin. But if we apostatize and (if) we deny (the Christ), we will distance ourselves from the Lord and the Sun of justice will not rise for us. As the prophet Isaiah says: * Behold, you yourself have been angry with us. As for us, we have sinned; that is why we have wandered; we have all become impure like a menstrual rag; all our righteousness (has disappeared); we have been cast away like leaves because of our sins; (our crimes) have carried us away like the wind. No one has invoked your name, nor has remembered you; that is why you have turned your face from us and you have delivered us to our sins. But now you yourself are, O Lord, our Father; we are mud; you yourself have created us and we are all the work of your hands; do not be angry with us anymore and do not remember our sins against us anymore; now also look at us, for we are your people. Thus says the Christ, King of glory: Those who did not seek me have found me and those who did not know me have invoked me. Moreover the prophet Isaiah says regarding those who do not believe in Christ: I have called them * and they have played deaf toward me; I have spoken to them and they have not answered me; they have done evil before me. Then the Christ will say to them: "Behold, you yourselves have plotted evil

against me and have outraged me. That is why you will weep, you will lament, you will groan from the bottom of your heart, you will become (beings) repudiated and scattered throughout the whole earth, and in addition you will remain in the fire of Gehenna until the ages of ages." 108. Repentance of Justus. - Having heard this, Justus wept and said: "O Our Lord and Our Savior Jesus Christ, Son of the living Lord, you are my Lord and my God. Forgive me my sins (as well as) the evil that I have uttered long ago against you, and do not abandon me. I have sinned against you; * do not bring your punishment upon me; do not repay me according to the malice of my works that I have done before you, and do not swallow me up in the fire of Gehenna, so that I do not stand in shame and reproach before your saints and your righteous ones on that great and dreadful day, for we have sinned, we have wandered, we have transgressed your Law and we have not kept your commandments. Behold, we have recognized the evil that our fathers have done against you. Moreover we have learned in your holy Gospel and by the mouth of your holy prophets how you were led like a sheep, to be slaughtered, how you were slapped, were crucified, died, were buried, and rose from the dead on the third day. All this happened to you in the flesh * that you took from us." Furthermore Justus said to Jacob: "Reveal to me, my brother, through the words of the

109. Jacob answers Justus's new questions. - Jacob answered and said to him: "Behold, I will explain to you, my brother, that, when we burned the church in the city of Acca, I myself entered the dwelling of the bishop and I found many Scriptures of the Old and New (Testament). Then I took the Old (Testament) and I burned the New in the fire. Moreover I found other books: * books of Basil, of Gregory, of John Chrysostom, of Abba Ephrem, of Epiphanius, (bishop) of Cyprus, and of Ambrose. Then I called a Christian, brought him to me, and said to him: 'What are these books?' Having seen (them), this man said to me: 'It is found written in them what the Christians believe. They say that the Christ is the Son of the Lord, (issued) from the divinity of the Father; that he was existing with him, before the world was created; that through him (the Father) made all creatures, without (the Christ) being separated from him; and that in the last days he was born of Mary, of the Holy Virgin, in the flesh.' Having heard this, I laughed and mocked him. Then when I understood

what was in the books that I read of the Holy * Fathers, wisdom and knowledge were revealed to me and I believed in Christ, Son of the living Lord."

110. How God became incarnate and suffered. - Justus says to Jacob: "You are blessed, my brother. Indeed, behold, the Lord has revealed to you a great mystery. Now also I want you to explain to me through the Scriptures of the Old (Testament) how Our Lord Jesus Christ descended from heaven; how he suffered in (his) body, endured all (kinds of) blows because of us, and received death in the flesh that he took from us; and how he became man, while he was God. If you do not reveal and elucidate this mystery, the Jews will find this pretext and will outrage the Christians." 111. Symbolism of the sacrifice of Isaac. - Jacob answered and said to him: "Behold, it is written in the Law: * The Lord said to Abraham: Take your son whom you love, Isaac; go into a high land; make him go up toward me and sacrifice him on a mountain that I will tell you. Abraham rose in the morning, harnessed his donkey, took two servants and Isaac, his son, made him go up on the mountain that the Lord showed him, and there bound him, in order to sacrifice him. (But) the Lord God called him and prevented him from bringing down the knife

on his son. Then he showed him a ram, suspended by its horns in a bush. Abraham took it and slaughtered it in place of Isaac, his son. Abraham is the type of the Lord Father; Isaac, for his part, is the image of the Son of the living Lord. As the Lord says * through the mouth of the prophet Isaiah regarding Christ: This is my Son whom I love (and) in whom my soul is pleased; I put my Spirit upon him. In him the Lord, the heavenly Father, is pleased. He will save his people from the worship of idols. As for the ram that was suspended in the bush (and) that was slaughtered in place of Isaac, it is the image of the flesh of Our Lord and Our Savior Jesus Christ who suffered, was crucified on the wood, and was pierced by the lance. The eternal Son, the Word of the Lord, endured suffering because of us. He was crucified on the wood of the cross, he received the sufferings (and) he died in the flesh that he took from us. But his divinity did not suffer. It is thus that Isaac was called a sacrifice, (but) it is not he who was slaughtered. (Abraham) took his two servants, that is to say * the two Laws: the Old and the New. The mountain that the Lord showed him, to sacrifice his son there, is the prophecy (announcing) that there would be crucified Our Lord Jesus Christ. Moreover our father Adam was buried in that place."

112. Symbolism of the story of Joseph. - Furthermore Joseph, son of Jacob, of whom his brothers were jealous, whom they sold for money and whom they threw into a pit where there was no water, is the image of what our fathers did against Christ, for they themselves are his brothers by the flesh. They sold him for thirty (pieces) of silver that Judas Iscariot received (who) betrayed him. As the prophet says: They took (for) the value of the Glorious one thirty (pieces) of silver, him whom they valued on the part of the children of Israel. The prophet Moses says: Cursed be he who receives a bribe, in order to kill the soul * (whose) blood (is) pure! All the people said: Amen. Amen. Justus says: "You have said a (thing that is) certain. Behold, I myself believe that the Christ is true God without change, (nor) corruption."
ተሠጥወ" ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ በከመ ፡፣ ሖረ ፣ ዮሴፍ ፡፣ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ አምድኅ ረ ፡ አውጽእዎ ፣ እምውስተ ፡ ግብ ፣ ዘአልቦ ፣ ማይ ፡ ወተሰይመ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ግብጻው ያን ፡፤ ወዲበ ፣ አሐዊሁ = ወከማሁ ፡ እግዚእነሂ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፤ ወሠአረ ፡፤ ጣዖተ ፡ ወአማሰነ ፡ አማልክቲሆሙ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡እ ምውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ ግብ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ማይ ፡ ተሰልጠ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ፍጥረ ት ፡ ወሰዓሮ ፡ ለገዲያብሎስ ፡ ወደምሰዕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ መሳእክቲሁ ፡፡
Jacob answered and said to him: "Just as Joseph went to the land of Egypt, after they had brought him out of the pit without water and he was placed over the Egyptians and over his brothers, likewise Our Lord Jesus Christ also entered the land of Egypt, destroyed the idols, and ruined their (false) gods. After he rose from the tomb, that is to say (from) the pit without water, he became powerful over all creatures, he destroyed the devil, and he annihilated him with all his angels."


[p. 88] {#po.13.p88}
ወዓዲ ፣ ይቤሎ ፡ እግ”ዚአብሔር ፡ ለኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እፍጥርከ ፡ በ ከርሰ ፣ እምከ ፡ አእመርኩከ ፡ ወዘእንበለ ፡ ትፃእ ፣ እማሕፀን ፡ ቀደስኩከ ፡ ወረሰይኩከ ፣ ነቢየ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ። ወካዕበ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሴምኩከ ፡ ዮም ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፡ ወለነገሥት ፡ ትሠርዎሙ ፣፡ ወትንሥኦሙ ፡ ወታጥፍኦሙ ፣ ወትሕንጽ ፡ ወትትክል = ወእምዝ ፡ አስ ተዋደይዎ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ቅትሎ ፡ ለዝ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢይነብ ብ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕዝብ ። ወዝሰ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ በእንተ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤልዎ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ለሏላጦስ ፣ ቅትሎ ፣ ለዝ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አስመ ፡ ኢይነብብ ፡ ሠናየ ‹፥ ላዕለ ፡ ሕግ ነ ፡ ወይጸርፍ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ኦሪትነ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፣ ሏላጦስ ፡ ንስእዎ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ወበከመ ፡ ሕግክሙ ፡ኩንንዎ =
113. Symbolism of the story of the prophet Jeremiah. - Moreover * the Lord says to the prophet Jeremiah: Before I had created you in the womb of your mother, I knew you, and before you had come out of the womb, I sanctified you and I established you as a prophet for the peoples. Moreover he says: Behold, I have established you today for the peoples and for the kings, in order that you extirpate them, remove them, and destroy them and (in order) that you build and that you plant. Then the Jews accused him and said to the king: Kill this man, for he does not speak good toward the people. This prophecy is regarding Christ. It is thus that the Jews said to Pilate: Kill this man, for he does not speak good toward our Law, (but) he blasphemes against our Law, and that Pilate said to them: Take him yourselves and judge him according to your Law."
ወበከመ ፣ ነሥእዎ ፡ ለኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡፣ ወወደይዎ ፣ ውስተ ፡ አዘቅት ፣"ኅበ፣0 አልቦ ፡ ማይ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይመስሎሙ ፡ ዘይመውት ፡ በህየ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አውጽእአዎ ፡ ሕያ ዎ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ይሙት ከማሁ ፡ አይሁድኒ ፡ ነሥእዎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶ ስ ፡ ሰቀልዎ ፡ ወወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ግብ ፡ሀበ ፡ አልቦ ፣ ማይ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ መቃብር ፡እን ዝ፣ ይመስሎሙ ፡ ዘኢይክል ፡ ተንሥኦ ፣ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። በ በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ክርስቶስ ። በደመ ፣ ሥርዐት ፣ ፈነውኩ ፡
Just as they took the prophet Jeremiah, and threw him into the well * where there was no water, while it seemed to them that he would die there, and that afterwards they brought him out alive, before he died, likewise the Jews took Our Lord Jesus Christ, crucified him, and threw him into the pit where there was no water, that is to say (into) the tomb, while it seemed to them that he could not rise. But he himself rose without corruption. As the prophet Zechariah says regarding Christ: By the blood of the Testament I have delivered the prisoners of the well without water. Likewise


[p. 89] {#po.13.p89}
ሙቁሓነ ፡ እምአዘቅት ፡ ዘአልቦ ፣ ማየ ፡= ወበከመ ፣ ኢሰምእዎዖ ፣ ለኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢ ይ ፡ ዘአዘዞሙ ፣ ወአምጽእ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ አምጽአ' ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ዋዌ ፡ ወመቅሰፍተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አለ ፡ ሰቀልዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወእሙ ንቱሰ ፣ ተዘርዉ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ዙሎሙ ፡ መንግሥታተ ፡ ሮም ፡ ወእስከ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ”ም
that they did not listen to the prophet Jeremiah who prescribed to them (the orders of the Lord) and brought upon them the punishment, just as the Lord brought upon them captivity and punishment: upon them who crucified Christ. As for them, they were scattered into all the kingdoms of Rome and even to the ends * of the earth.

114. Symbolism of the story of the prophet Daniel. - Likewise the prophet Daniel, they threw him into the lions' den, so that he might die. Now behold, he himself did not die, but on the contrary, when the lions had seen him, they were afraid, were terrified, and moved away from him. This prophecy is concerning Christ. When they had laid him in the tomb, his flesh did not see corruption (but) confounded his enemies. When Sheol had seen him, it was afraid and was terrified. (Christ) broke Satan and tore his weapons (from him). As the prophet David says: The Lord will break their teeth in their mouth and will tear out the molars of the lions; they will become vile like water that is poured out. He will stretch their bow to the point of making it weak. The prophet Job says: Have the gates of death been opened for you because of your majesty, and have the porters of Sheol been terrified, * as soon as they saw you?
ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ኮንኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንጋዔ ፡ በቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ወተሳለቁ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡=፡ ከማ
115. Christ as an object of mockery. - The prophet says: I have become

ሁ ፡ ገብሩ ፤ አበዊነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወተሳለቁ ፡ ላዕሴሁ ። በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ፈተኑኒ ፡ ወተሳለቁ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፣ ወሰሐቁኒ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እፄ ፣፡ ወአ ኮ ፡ ሰብእ = ምኑን ፣ በኀበ ፣ ሰብእ ፣ ወትሁት ፣ በውስተ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡= ኩሎሙ ፡ አለ ይሬእዩኒ ፣ ይትቃጸቡኒ ፣ ይብሉ ፣ በከናፍሪሆሙ ፡ ወያሐውሱ ፡፣ ርአሶዕሙ ። ተወከ ለ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ ለያድኅኖ ፡= ወያድኅኖ ፡ አመ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ከመዝ ፡- ገብሩ፣ አበዊነ ፣ ላዕለ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ አመ ፡ ይሰቅልዎ ፡ አኖሥሠ ፡፣ ርእሶሙ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፤ አመ ሰ፣ ያፈቅሮ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያድኅኖ ፣ ወይርድኦ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ሐረዮ ፣ እስመ ፣ ውእ ቱ ፡ይቤ ፣ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አነ ፡= እስመ ፣ በእን"ተዝ ፡፣ ተነበየ ፡ ዳዊት ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡፡
like a madness before them and they mocked me. Likewise our fathers acted toward Christ and mocked him. As the prophet David says: They tested me; they mocked me and they laughed at me. Furthermore he says: I am a worm and not a man; I am rejected among men and humiliated among the people. All those who see me blink (their eyes) at me, say with their lips and shake their heads: He trusted in the Lord. Let him save him! Let him save him, if he wants. This is how our fathers acted toward Christ, when they crucified him. They shook their heads and they said: If the Lord loves him, let him save him! Let him rescue him, if he chose him! Indeed, he himself said: I am the Son of the Lord. That is why * David prophesied about them.
ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ አነሥአ ፡ እደውየ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ወኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ይቤ ፡፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ እሰፍሕ ፡ እደውየ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ዓላውያን ፡ ወከሓድያን' ፡ እለ ፡ ኢሐሖሩ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወተለዉ ፡ ኅጢአቶሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ዘአምዕዑኒ፣ ወ ሦዑ ፡ ወዐጠኑ ፡ ለአጋንንት ፡፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አንሰ፣ ኢይክሕድሂ ፡ ወኢይትዋቀስሂ ፡ ወመጠውኩ" ፡ ዘባንየ ፡ ለመቅሠፍት ፡ ወመልታሕትየ ፡ ለጽፍዐት ፡ ወኢሜጥኩ ፡ ገጽ
116. Christ as the victim of sinners. - Furthermore he says: All day long, I lift my hands toward you. The prophet Isaiah says: All day long, I stretch out my hands toward a people of perverse and unfaithful ones who have not walked (in) the path of justice, (but) have followed their sin. It is this people who irritated me; they sacrificed and offered incense to demons. Furthermore he says: As for me, I did not refuse and I did not contest, (but) I gave my back to the blows and my cheeks to the slaps and I did not turn my face away from the ignominy of spitting.


[p. 91] {#po.13.p91}

The prophet David says: Upon my back sinners have struck and have stretched out their sins. Furthermore the prophet Isaiah says: He came, in order to be slaughtered like a lamb: he was like a lamb before the one who shears him; thus he did not open his mouth in his suffering. Have you understood, my brother, that the prophets of old * knew and mentioned the passion of Our Lord and Our Savior Jesus Christ, his crucifixion, his death, his resurrection, his ascension into the heavens and his second coming in great glory? 117. On Muhammad. - Justus answered and said to him: "As for me, I believe in Christ, Son of the living Lord; (I believe) that he is the salvation of all (men) and that there is no other God than him. Blessed is he who believes in him. Behold, I (am going to) tell you, O my brother Jacob, that my brother Abraham sent me a letter to Caesarea and said to me: "Behold, a prophet has been sent to the Arabs at the city of Mecca and he has killed Sargis of Qandart." Having learned (this), the Jews rejoiced enormously. Then I went myself to a wise old man, intelligent (and) instructed in the Law and the prophets; I questioned him and said to him: "Explain to me what is the preaching of this prophet?"
ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ መስሕት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ ነ”ቢየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ነቢያትሰ ፡ ኢይትኔበዩ ፣ በመጥባሕት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ዳአሙ ፣ ዘይሜህሩ ፣ ፍኖተ ፣ ጽድቅ ፣ ወሕይወ
He answered and said to me: "He is a seducer; he is not a * prophet, for the prophets, they do not prophesy by the sword, but on the contrary,

They teach the way of justice and of life. Now as for me, I think that (Jesus is) the Christ who has come. The Christians believe in him, but the Jews deny him. I say that he himself is therefore the true Christ." 118. Confession of Justus. - Having heard this, I thanked and glorified (the Lord) and I believed in Christ who came and was born of Mary, the Holy Virgin, in Bethlehem of Judah, 69 weeks after the (re)construction of the temple, (carried out) after the return from the Babylonian captivity. Behold, I myself have believed and I have understood that he is the Christ about whom the prophets prophesied, (and) that he is the King of Israel who has come for the salvation of the whole world." Jacob answered and said to him: "Watch, O my brother Justus, not to think contempt in your heart, (but) with your lips to express justice." * Justus said to him: "The Lord, God of Moses, knows that with all my heart and all my intelligence I believe in Christ, Son of the living Lord, in whom the Christians believe.” 119. Justus declares to Jacob that he wants to go and convert his own people. - Jacob answered and said to him: "Rise, let us go to (my) house and let us make the agape." Then he took Justus, brought him into his house, presented the table to him and said to him: "Bless, my brother." Justus said to him: "It is for you that it belongs to bless." Jacob said: "May Christ bless!" Justus said: "Amen."


[p. 93] {#po.13.p93}

Justus said to him: "Do you want to test me? The Lord knows that I myself with all my heart have believed in Christ through your own teaching. Behold, I have seen in a dream, for many nights, a man who was clothed in white garments and was saying to me: "Everything that Jacob has expressed is true; there is no lie (in him), for * the grace of the Holy Spirit dwells upon him." Jacob answered and said to him: "Since you have known and understood (with certainty) all this, how then are you not baptized, so that you may manifest the faith of the Christians?" Justus said to him: "As for me, I want to go to my country to convert my relatives to the faith in Christ and to be baptized myself jointly with them."
ወአውሥአ ፡ ያፅቆብ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ እፈርሕ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እንዳኢ ፡ እመ ፡ ኢያስ ሐትከ"፣ አዝማዲከ፣ ወያስተሐቅሩከ ፡ ወኢያበውሑከ ፡ ትጠመቅ ፡ እስመ ፡ አይሁድሰ፣ ጽሉላነ ፡ ልብ ፡ እሙንቱ ።፡ በከመ ፡ ኢሳጳቪይገያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፤ ወገዝፈ ፣ ልቦሙ ፡ ለዝን ቱ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዮስጥስ ፡ ሕያው ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኩነኑኒ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወቀሠፉኒ ፡ እመ ፡ ኢተመየጥኩ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወአመ ፣፤ ኢክሕደክቻ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ እግዚእየ ፣ ወ አምላኪየ ፳ እስመ ፡ አኮ ፡ በትምሕርተ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፣ ዘአመንኩ ፡ "ዘእንበለ ፡ ዳ
Jacob answered and said to him: "Behold, as for me, I fear greatly that you will not persuade your relatives. They will despise you and they will not allow you to be baptized, for the Jews, they are darkened in heart. As (says) the prophet Isaiah: The heart of this people has become thick." Justus said: "The Lord is living! The Jews have tortured me and have scourged me, since I did not convert to them and since I did not deny Christ, my Lord and my God. Indeed, it is not only through your own teaching that I have believed, * but rather (by the word of) the one who appeared to me

in a dream and led me to the faith. Now I ask you to teach me the prayers that you have learned from the Christians." 120. Jacob teaches Justus the prayers of the Creed and the Pater. - Then Jacob taught him the prayer of faith [the Creed] and the prayer of the Gospel that Our Lord taught his disciples [the Pater noster]. Immediately Justus wanted to go away to his country. Jacob blessed him and said to him: "May the grace of the Holy Spirit be with you during all the days of your life and may it lead you in the path of justice!"
ወእምዝ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ዮስጥስ ፡ በጥበብ ፣ ኩሎ ፡ በኀበ ፡፣ ዮሴፍ ፣ ኩሎ ፣ ዘጸሐፈ፣ ስምዖን ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተባሀሉ ፡ አኀው ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወዘከመሂ ፡ ተሰጥወ ፡ ያ ፅቆብ ፡ ይነግሮሙ ፡፣ ትንቢተ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ስምዐ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ =፡ ወሶ በ ፣ፈቀደ"፣ዮስጥስ ፡ይዕረግ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ተኣምሁ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ በአምሓ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ብካይ ፡=፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ "ዮስጥስ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፡ ጸሊ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ኦጌር ፡ መምሕር ፡ እስ መ ፡ በትምሕርተ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ አእመርክዖ ፡ ለብርሃነ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አም ላኪየ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አበሳየ ፡ ወኀጢአትየ ፡ ዘገበርኩ ፡ ቅድሜሁ" ፡=፡ ተሰጥ
121. Departure of Justus. - Then Justus wrote with wisdom all (these things) at Joseph's (and) everything that Simeon, the son (of the latter, had written, namely): how the brothers had argued among themselves and how also Jacob had answered, (by) exposing to them the prophecies of the prophets, (as) testimony concerning the coming of Christ. When Justus wanted to board the ship, they kissed each other with kisses of holiness, with many tears. Then * Justus said to Jacob: "Pray for me, O eminent doctor, for it is through your own teaching that I have known the Light of justice, that is to say Christ, my God, so that he may forgive me my faults and my sins that I have committed before him." Jacob answered and said to him: "May the Lord


[p. 95] {#po.13.p95}
ወ ፡ ያፅቆብ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ይስረይ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢይዘከር ፣ አበሳከ ፡ ወያድኅኖ ሙ ፡ ለአዝማዲከ ፡ ወለሰብአ ፡ ቤትከ ፡ ለአለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በክርስቶስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ዐርገ ፡ ዮስጥስ ፡ ሐመረ ፡ ወጸንዐ ፡ በአሚነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚ ኦብሔር ፡ ሕያው = ወአንሰ ፡ ዮሌፍ ፡ ሓጥእ ፡ ወአባሲ ፡ ዘአጽሐፍኩ፤ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ መጽሐፈ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘ
(forgive) you, may he no longer remember your faults and may he save your relatives and the people of your house who will believe in Christ!" Then Justus boarded (the) ship. He was strengthened in the faith of Christ, Son of the living Lord. It is I, Joseph, sinner and criminal, who have had this book written (and) everything about which the brothers argued among themselves.
ተባሀሉ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ አኀው ፡፡ ወናሁ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ያዕቆብ ፣ በሕልም ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ወሰምአ ፡ ቃለ ፣ ዘይብሎ ፡ ፃእ ፡ ኦያዕ ቆብ ፡ እማእከለ ፡ ሰብለ ፣ ወዓሥሥ ፡፣ ጾመ ፡ ወጸሎተ ፡ ወብካየ ፣ ላዕለ፣ ኀጣውኢከ፣ ዘገበርከ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን" ፡=፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ወጽአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እማእከለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በአታት ፡ ወግበ በ፣ምድር ፡እንዘ ፡ ይትቀነይ ፣፡ ለአግዚአብሔር ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወሐይወ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ፡=፡ ወንሕነኒ ፣ ንስአሎ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወመድኀኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ይስረይ ፡ ኅጢ አተነ ፡ ወይደምስስ ፡ ጌጋየነ፤፣ ወኀጢአተነ፣ ወኢ[ይ]ዝክር ፡ አበሳነ ፡ ወጌጋየነ ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወመለኮት ፡ ወክብር ፡፣ ይእዜኒ ፡ ወለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፣ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ፡=
122. End of the life of Jacob. - Behold, Jacob saw in a dream a man and he heard a voice that said to him: "Go out, O Jacob, from the midst of men and seek fasting, prayer and tears for your sins (and for) * the evil that you have done to the Church." Then Jacob went out from the midst of men and went into the caves and caverns of the earth, serving the Lord, during all the days of his life, through fasting and through prayer. He lived in beautiful piety and died in peace. As for us, let us ask Our Lord and Our Savior Jesus Christ to forgive (us) our sins, to erase our crimes and our sins and to no longer remember our faults and our crimes, - to whom be glory, divinity and honor now and for ages of ages! Amen. Amen.
